## Heavy-duty Vehicle and Engine Greenhouse Gas Emission Regulations {#heavy-duty-vehicle-and-engine-greenhouse-gas-emission-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2013-24
:::

[CANADIAN ENVIRONMENTAL PROTECTION ACT, 1999](/eng/acts/C-15.31)

Registration 2013-02-22

Heavy-duty Vehicle and Engine Greenhouse Gas Emission Regulations

P.C. 2013-160 2013-02-22
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Whereas, pursuant to subsection 332(1)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_a){#fn_a-ID0EICBA} of the [Canadian
Environmental Protection Act, 1999](/eng/acts/C-15.31)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_b){#fn_b-ID0EFCBA}, the Minister of the
Environment published in the [Canada
Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/), Part I, on April 14, 2012, a copy
of the proposed Heavy-duty Vehicle and Engine Greenhouse Gas Emission
Regulations, substantially in the annexed form, and persons were given
an opportunity to file comments with respect to the Regulations or to
file a notice of objection requesting that a board of review be
established and stating the reasons for the objection;

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote ]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_a-ID0EICBA){#fn_a}S.C.
  2004, c. 15, s. 31
  :::

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote ]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_b-ID0EFCBA){#fn_b}S.C.
  1999, c. 33
  :::

Therefore, His Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the
recommendation of the Minister of the Environment, pursuant to sections
160 and 162 of the [Canadian Environmental Protection Act,
1999](/eng/acts/C-15.31)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_b){#fn_b-ID0EDAAA}, makes the annexed
Heavy-duty Vehicle and Engine Greenhouse Gas Emission Regulations.
:::

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-800450 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in these Regulations.

  [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Canadian Environmental
      Protection Act, 1999](/eng/acts/C-15.31). ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[adjusted loaded vehicle weight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[adjusted loaded vehicle weight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      numerical average of the curb weight and the GVWR, and in the case
      of vehicles referred to in subsection 26(6) with an adjusted
      loaded vehicle weight of more than 6 350 kg (14,000 pounds), the
      value corresponding to the nearest 225 kg (500 pounds)
      increment. ([poids ajusté du véhicule chargé]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[aftertreatment device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[aftertreatment device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a catalytic
      converter, particulate filter or any other system or component
      mounted downstream of the exhaust valve or exhaust port that is
      designed to decrease engine exhaust emissions before they are
      released into the environment. ([dispositif de traitement
      postcombustion]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[A to B testing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[A to B testing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means testing performed in
      pairs to allow comparison of a vehicle A to a vehicle B, an engine
      A to an engine B or equipment A to equipment B, as the case may
      be. ([essais A à B]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[automatic tire inflation system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[automatic tire inflation system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      pneumatically or electronically activated system that is installed
      on a vehicle or a trailer to maintain tire pressure at a specified
      value. ([dispositif automatique de gonflage des
      pneus]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[auxiliary emission control device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[auxiliary emission control device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      any element of design that senses temperature, vehicle speed,
      engine RPM, transmission gear, manifold vacuum, or any other
      parameter for the purpose of activating, modulating, delaying or
      deactivating the operation of any part of an emission control
      system. ([dispositif antipollution auxiliaire]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[averaging set]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[averaging set]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, for the purpose of a
      company's participation in the CO~2~ emission credit system set
      out in sections 34 to 47 or sections 47.1 to 47.5, as the case may
      be, any of the following groups of fleets of vehicles, engines or
      trailers:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty vehicles and
        cab-complete vehicles, excluding those that are vocational
        vehicles or incomplete vocational vehicles;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} vocational vehicles that are light heavy-duty
        vehicles and incomplete vocational vehicles that are to become
        light heavy-duty vehicles;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} medium heavy-duty vehicles and heavy-duty
        incomplete vehicles that are to become medium heavy-duty
        vehicles, other than those that are of a type referred to in the
        table to subsection 26(1.2) and in respect of which the company
        has made the election referred to in that subsection;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} heavy heavy-duty vehicles and heavy-duty
        incomplete vehicles that are to become heavy heavy-duty
        vehicles, other than those that are of a type referred to in the
        table to subsection 26(1.2) and in respect of which the company
        has made the election referred to in that subsection;

      - [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} vocational vehicles that are all of the same
        type referred to in the table to subsection 26(1.2) and in
        respect of which the company has made the election referred to
        in that subsection;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} light heavy-duty engines and medium heavy-duty
        engines that are spark-ignition engines;

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} light heavy-duty engines that are
        compression-ignition engines;

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} medium heavy-duty engines that are
        compression-ignition engines;

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} heavy heavy-duty engines;

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} full-aero box van trailers that are short dry
        box van trailers and full-aero box van trailers that are short
        refrigerated box van trailers; or

      - [(j)]{.lawlabel} full-aero box van trailers that are long dry
        box van trailers and full-aero box van trailers that are long
        refrigerated box van trailers. ([groupe de calcul de
        points]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[basic vehicle frontal area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[basic vehicle frontal area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the area
      enclosed by the geometric projection of the basic vehicle ---
      including tires but not mirrors or air deflectors --- along the
      longitudinal axis of the vehicle onto a plane perpendicular to
      that axis. ([surface frontale du véhicule de
      base]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[box van trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[box van trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trailer --- other
      than a tank trailer that is designed to transport liquids or gases
      --- that has an enclosed cargo space that is permanently attached
      to the trailer chassis, has fixed sides and a fixed nose and
      roof. ([remorque fourgon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[brake horsepower]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[brake horsepower]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
      [[BHP]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a unit of brake power that is
      equal to 745.7 watts, expressed in
      horsepower. ([BHP]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[bus]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[bus]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vocational vehicle that is
      designed to carry more than 15
      passengers. ([autobus]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[cab-complete vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cab-complete vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a heavy-duty
      incomplete vehicle with either a completed occupant compartment
      that requires only the addition of a cargo-carrying surface,
      work-performing equipment or load-bearing component to perform its
      intended functions or with the back of the cab cut out for the
      intended installation of a structure that permits access from the
      driver's area to the back of the vehicle. ([véhicule à cabine
      complète]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[calibration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[calibration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the set of
      specifications and tolerances specific to a particular design,
      version or application of a component or assembly that describes
      its operation over its working
      range. ([calibrages]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CFR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CFR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Code of Federal Regulations
      of the United States, as amended from time to
      time. ([CFR]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CH~4~]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CH~4~]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      methane. ([CH~4~]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Class 2B]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Class 2B]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a class of heavy-duty
      vehicle that has a GVWR of more than 3 856 kg (8,500 pounds) but
      not more than 4 536 kg (10,000 pounds). ([classe
      2B]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Class 3]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Class 3]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a class of heavy-duty
      vehicle that has a GVWR of more than 4 536 kg (10,000 pounds) but
      not more than 6 350 kg (14,000 pounds). ([classe
      3]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Class 4]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Class 4]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a class of heavy-duty
      vehicle that has a GVWR of more than 6 350 kg (14,000 pounds) but
      not more than 7 257 kg (16,000 pounds). ([classe
      4]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Class 5]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Class 5]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a class of heavy-duty
      vehicle that has a GVWR of more than 7 257 kg (16,000 pounds) but
      not more than 8 845 kg (19,500 pounds). ([classe
      5]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Class 6]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Class 6]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a class of heavy-duty
      vehicle that has a GVWR of more than 8 845 kg (19,500 pounds) but
      not more than 11 793 kg (26,000 pounds). ([classe
      6]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Class 7]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Class 7]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a class of heavy-duty
      vehicle that has a GVWR of more than 11 793 kg (26,000 pounds) but
      not more than 14 969 kg (33,000 pounds). ([classe
      7]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Class 8]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Class 8]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a class of heavy-duty
      vehicle that has a GVWR of more than 14 969 kg (33,000
      pounds). ([classe 8]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[coach bus]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[coach bus]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bus that is designed for
      intercity passenger transport and that is not equipped with
      features to accommodate standing
      passengers. ([autocar]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[compression-ignition engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[compression-ignition engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      engine that operates as a reciprocating internal combustion
      engine, but does not include an engine that operates under
      characteristics significantly similar to the theoretical Otto
      combustion cycle or an engine that uses a spark plug or other
      sparking device. ([moteur à allumage par
      compression]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[concrete mixer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[concrete mixer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vocational vehicle
      that is designed to mix and transport concrete in a revolving drum
      that is permanently mounted. ([bétonnière]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[CO~2~]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CO~2~]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means carbon
      dioxide. ([CO~2~]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CO~2~ family certification level]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CO~2~ family certification level]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in
      respect of a company's heavy-duty engines, means the maximum CO~2~
      emission level determined by the company for a fleet, which is
      greater than or equal to the maximum CO~2~ deteriorated emission
      level value calculated in accordance with subsection 32(1) for the
      engines that are included in the fleet. ([niveau de certification
      de la famille applicable au CO~2~]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[curb weight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[curb weight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the actual or
      manufacturer's estimated weight of a heavy-duty vehicle in
      operational status with all standard equipment and includes the
      weight of fuel at nominal tank capacity and the weight of optional
      equipment. ([masse en état de marche]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[day cab]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[day cab]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a tractor cab that is not a
      sleeper cab. ([cabine de jour]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[deteriorated emission level]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[deteriorated emission level]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      emission level that results from applying the applicable
      deterioration factor to the emission test results for a vehicle or
      engine. ([niveau d'émissions détérioré]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[deterioration factor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[deterioration factor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the factor used
      to account for the variation, if any, between the maximum emission
      level during the useful life of a vehicle or engine and the
      undeteriorated emission level measured at the point corresponding
      to a maximum of 6 437 km (4,000 miles) of operation in relation to
      a vehicle that has stabilized emissions and to a maximum of 125
      hours of operation in relation to an engine that has stabilized
      emissions, determined in accordance with

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} sections 1819(d)(5) and 1823(m) of Title 40,
        chapter I, subchapter C, part 86, subpart S, of the CFR, in the
        case of Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty vehicles and
        cab-complete vehicles, excluding those that are vocational
        vehicles or incomplete vocational vehicles;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} section 241(c) of Title 40, chapter I,
        subchapter U, part 1037, subpart C, of the CFR, in the case of
        vocational vehicles, incomplete vocational vehicles, tractors
        and incomplete tractors; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} section 150(g) of Title 40, chapter I,
        subchapter U, part 1036, subpart B, of the CFR, and section
        241(c) of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1036, subpart
        C, of the CFR, in the case of heavy-duty engines. ([facteur de
        détérioration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[dry box van trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[dry box van trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a box van
      trailer other than a refrigerated box van trailer. ([remorque
      fourgon non frigorifique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[electric vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[electric vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a heavy-duty
      vehicle, other than a fuel cell vehicle, that is not equipped with
      an internal combustion engine and is powered solely by an external
      source of electricity or solar power or a combination of
      electricity and solar power. ([véhicule
      électrique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[element of design]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[element of design]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a
      vehicle, engine or trailer,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any control system, including computer
        software, electronic control systems and computer logic;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any control system calibrations;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the results of systems interaction; or

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any hardware items. ([élément de
        conception]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[emergency vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[emergency vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vocational
      vehicle that is designed for use as an ambulance or fire
      truck. ([véhicule d'urgence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[emission control system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[emission control system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any emission
      control device, auxiliary emission control device, engine
      modification and strategy, and other element of design used to
      reduce exhaust emissions from a vehicle or engine. ([système
      antipollution]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[engine configuration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[engine configuration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a unique
      combination of heavy-duty engine hardware and calibration that has
      an effect on measured emissions within an engine family or engine
      subfamily, as the case may be. ([configuration de
      moteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[engine family]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[engine family]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a company's
      heavy-duty engines other than those referred to in section 25,
      means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if they are covered by an EPA certificate, the
        grouping for which the EPA certificate was issued; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if they are not covered by an EPA certificate,
        the grouping determined in accordance with section 230 of Title
        40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1036, subpart C, of the
        CFR. ([famille de moteurs]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[engine subfamily]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[engine subfamily]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a
      company's heavy-duty engines other than those referred to in
      section 25, means the grouping within an engine family having the
      same CO~2~ family certification level, determined in accordance
      with section 230 of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1036,
      subpart C, of the CFR. ([sous-famille de moteurs]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[EPA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[EPA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the United States Environmental
      Protection Agency. ([EPA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[EPA certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[EPA certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a certificate of
      conformity with U.S. federal standards issued by the
      EPA. ([certificat de l'EPA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[family emission limit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[family emission limit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of CO~2~ emissions,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a company's vocational vehicles
          and incomplete vocational vehicles, the maximum CO~2~ emission
          level determined by the company for a fleet or subfleet, which
          is greater than or equal to the maximum CO~2~ emission rate
          determined in accordance with subsection 26(2) for the
          vocational vehicles and incomplete vocational vehicles that
          are included in the fleet or subfleet,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a company's tractors and
          incomplete tractors, the maximum CO~2~ emission level
          determined by the company for a fleet or subfleet, which is
          greater than or equal to the maximum CO~2~ emission rate
          determined in accordance with subsection 27(2) for the
          tractors and incomplete tractors that are included in the
          fleet or subfleet, and

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a company's full-aero box van
          trailers, the maximum CO~2~ emission level determined by the
          company for a subfleet, which is greater than or equal to the
          maximum CO~2~ emission rate determined in accordance with
          subsection 33.1(3) for the trailers that are included in the
          subfleet; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of N~2~O or CH~4~ emissions,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a company's Class 2B and Class
          3 heavy-duty vehicles and cab-complete vehicles --- excluding
          those that are vocational vehicles or incomplete vocational
          vehicles --- the maximum N~2~O or CH~4~ emission level
          determined by the company for a fleet or subfleet, which is
          greater than or equal to the maximum N~2~O or CH~4~ emission
          value determined in accordance with section 24 for the
          vehicles or vehicle configurations that are included in the
          fleet or subfleet, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a company's heavy-duty
          engines, the maximum N~2~O or CH~4~ emission level determined
          by the company for a fleet, which is greater than or equal to
          the maximum N~2~O or CH~4~ deteriorated emission level value
          calculated using the applicable emission value determined in
          accordance with subsection 29(2) for the engine configurations
          that are included in the fleet. ([limite d'émissions de la
          famille]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[FTP-based city test]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[FTP-based city test]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Federal Test
      Procedure set out in section 127 of Title 40, chapter I,
      subchapter C, part 86, subpart B, of the CFR, to comply with the
      FTP emission standards. ([essai en ville]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[fuel cell vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fuel cell vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a heavy-duty
      vehicle propelled solely by an electric motor, the energy for the
      motor being supplied by an electrochemical cell that produces
      electricity via the non-combustion reaction of a consumable fuel,
      typically hydrogen. ([véhicule à pile à
      combustible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[full-aero]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[full-aero]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a box van
      trailer, means that the trailer does not have any of the following
      features:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a belly box that occupies at least half of the
        length of both sides of the trailer between the centreline of
        the landing gear when it is extended and the leading edge of the
        front wheels;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a drop deck;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a side-mounted, pull-out platform;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} one or more steps for side-door access;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a side lift gate;

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a rear hinged loading ramp;

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a rear mount for a forklift;

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a rear lift gate or, for the 2023 model year or
        an earlier model year, a roll-up rear door. ([totalement
        aérodynamique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[GAWR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[GAWR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the gross axle weight rating
      that is specified by a manufacturer as the load-carrying capacity
      of a single axle system, as measured at the tire-ground
      interface. ([PNBE]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[GCWR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[GCWR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the gross combination weight
      rating that is specified by a manufacturer as the maximum design
      loaded weight of a vehicle and trailer. ([PNBC]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[GEM computer simulation model]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[GEM computer simulation model]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      EPA's GEM computer simulation model referred to in section 520 of
      Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1037, subpart F, of the
      CFR. ([modèle de simulation informatique GEM]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[GVWR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[GVWR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the gross vehicle weight rating
      that is specified by a manufacturer as the maximum design loaded
      weight of a vehicle. ([PNBV]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[heavy-duty completed vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[heavy-duty completed vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2018-98, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[heavy-duty engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[heavy-duty engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an engine that is
      designed to be used for motive power in a vocational vehicle or a
      tractor. ([moteur de véhicule lourd]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[heavy-duty incomplete vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[heavy-duty incomplete vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      heavy-duty vehicle, an on-road vehicle with a curb weight of more
      than 2 722 kg (6,000 pounds) or an on-road vehicle with a basic
      vehicle frontal area in excess of 4.2 m^2^ (45 square feet) that
      consists of, at a minimum, a chassis, a powertrain and wheels in
      the state in which all of those components are to be part of the
      vehicle, but that requires further manufacturing operations to be
      completed. ([véhicule lourd incomplet]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[heavy-duty vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[heavy-duty vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an on-road
      vehicle that has a GVWR of more than 3 856 kg (8,500 pounds) but
      does not include a [[medium-duty passenger
      vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 1(1) of
      the On-Road Vehicle and Engine Emission Regulations or a vehicle
      regulated under the [Passenger Automobile and Light Truck
      Greenhouse Gas Emission
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2010-201). ([véhicule
      lourd]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[heavy-haul tractor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[heavy-haul tractor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a tractor of the
      2021 model year or a subsequent model year that has a GCWR of
      63 503 kg (140,000 pounds) or more. ([tracteur routier à
      chargement lourd]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[heavy heavy-duty engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[heavy heavy-duty engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a heavy-duty
      engine that has cylinder liners designed for multiple rebuilds and
      is designed to be used in Class 8 heavy-duty vehicles. ([gros
      moteur de véhicule lourd]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[heavy heavy-duty vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[heavy heavy-duty vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the 2020 model year and earlier model
        years, a Class 8 heavy-duty vehicle; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the 2021 model year and subsequent model
        years,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a Class 6, Class 7 or Class 8 vocational
          vehicle that is equipped with a heavy heavy-duty engine,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a Class 8 vocational vehicle that is an
          electric vehicle,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a Class 8 vocational vehicle that is a fuel
          cell vehicle,

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a Class 8 vocational vehicle that is a
          hybrid vehicle, or

        - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a Class 8 tractor. ([gros véhicule
          lourd]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[HFET-based highway test]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[HFET-based highway test]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Highway
      Fuel Economy Test Procedure referred to in subpart B of Title 40,
      chapter I, subchapter Q, part 600, of the CFR. ([essai sur
      route]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[high-roof]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[high-roof]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a tractor, means
      having a roof height of 376 cm (148 inches) or more. ([toit
      élevé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[hybrid engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hybrid engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or [[hybrid
      powertrain]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an engine or a powertrain
      that is equipped with energy storage features --- other than a
      conventional battery system or conventional flywheel --- such as
      supplemental electric batteries and hydraulic
      accumulators. ([moteur hybride]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
      [groupe motopropulseur hybride]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[hybrid vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hybrid vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a heavy-duty vehicle
      that is equipped with energy storage features --- other than a
      conventional battery system or conventional flywheel --- such as
      supplemental electric batteries and hydraulic accumulators, in
      addition to an internal combustion engine or other engine that
      uses fuel. ([véhicule hybride]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[incomplete tractor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[incomplete tractor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a heavy-duty
      incomplete vehicle that is designed to become a tractor on
      completion of manufacturing operations. ([tracteur routier
      incomplet]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[incomplete vocational vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[incomplete vocational vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      heavy-duty incomplete vehicle that is designed to become a
      vocational vehicle on completion of manufacturing
      operations. ([véhicule spécialisé incomplet]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[innovative technology]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[innovative technology]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a greenhouse
      gas emission reduction technology for which the total emission
      reduction attributable to it cannot be measured by either GEM
      computer simulation modelling or the test procedures specified
      under these Regulations. ([technologie
      innovatrice]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[light heavy-duty engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[light heavy-duty engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a heavy-duty
      engine that is designed to be used in Class 2B, Class 3, Class 4
      or Class 5 heavy-duty vehicles. ([petit moteur de véhicule
      lourd]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[light heavy-duty vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[light heavy-duty vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Class 2B,
      Class 3, Class 4 or Class 5 heavy-duty vehicle. ([petit véhicule
      lourd]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[long]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[long]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a trailer, means more
      than 15.24 metres (50 feet) in length. ([longue]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[low-roof]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[low-roof]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a tractor, means
      having a roof height of 305 cm (120 inches) or less. ([toit
      bas]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[medium heavy-duty engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[medium heavy-duty engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      heavy-duty engine that is designed to be used in Class 6 and Class
      7 heavy-duty vehicles. ([moteur moyen de véhicule
      lourd]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[medium heavy-duty vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[medium heavy-duty vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the 2020 model year and earlier model
        years, a Class 6 or Class 7 heavy-duty vehicle; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the 2021 model year and subsequent model
        years,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a Class 6, Class 7 or Class 8 vocational
          vehicle that is not a hybrid vehicle and is equipped with an
          engine other than a heavy heavy-duty engine,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a Class 6 or Class 7 vocational vehicle that
          is an electric vehicle,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a Class 6 or Class 7 vocational vehicle
          that is a fuel cell vehicle,

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a Class 6 or Class 7 vocational vehicle that
          is a hybrid vehicle, or

        - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a Class 7 tractor. ([véhicule
          mi-lourd]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[mid-roof]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mid-roof]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a tractor, means
      having a roof height of more than 305 cm (120 inches) but less
      than 376 cm (148 inches). ([toit moyen]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[mixed-use vocational vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mixed-use vocational vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      vocational vehicle that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is designed to operate at low speeds that are
        unsuitable for normal highway operation, has affixed components
        that are designed to operate in an off-road environment or has
        both of those characteristics; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has one or more of the following
        characteristics:

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it has an axle that has a GAWR of 13 154 kg
          (29,000 pounds) or more,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} it cannot attain a speed of more than
          53 km/h (33 miles per hour) over 3.2 km (2 miles),

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} it cannot attain a speed of more than
          72 km/h (45 miles per hour) over 3.2 km (2 miles), it has an
          unloaded vehicle weight that is not less than 95% of its GVWR
          and it cannot carry occupants other than the driver and
          operating crew,

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} it cannot attain a speed of more than
          87 km/h (54 miles per hour)

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} due to parameters that are not [[adjustable
            parameters]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection
            15(1), or

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} without the engine with which it is
            equipped being at 95% or more of its maximum test speed in
            the highest available gear ratio. ([véhicule spécialisé à
            usages divers]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[model year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[model year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the year, determined in
      accordance with section 4, that is used by a manufacturer to
      designate a model of vehicle, engine or trailer. ([année de
      modèle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[motor home]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[motor home]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vocational vehicle that
      is designed to provide temporary residential accommodations and
      that is equipped with at least four of the following features:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a cooking appliance;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a refrigerator;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a self-contained toilet;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a heating or air-conditioning system;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a potable water supply system, including a
        faucet and a sink;

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a 110-125 volt alternating current (VAC)
        electrical supply system or propane supply
        system. ([autocaravane]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[multi-purpose vocational vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[multi-purpose vocational vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      vocational vehicle that is neither a regional vocational vehicle
      nor an urban vocational vehicle. ([véhicule spécialisé à usages
      multiples]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[nominal tank capacity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[nominal tank capacity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the fuel
      tank's volume that is specified by a manufacturer to the nearest
      three eighths of a litre (one tenth of a U.S. gallon). ([capacité
      nominale du réservoir à carburant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[non-aero box van trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[non-aero box van trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a box van trailer that is at least 10.67 metres
        (35 feet) in length and that has

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} at least one of the following rear features:

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} a hinged loading ramp,

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} a lift gate,

          - [(C)]{.lawlabel} a mount for a forklift,

          - [(D)]{.lawlabel} for the 2023 model year or an earlier model
            year, a roll-up door, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} at least one of the following side features:

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} a belly box that occupies at least half of
            the length of both sides of the trailer between the
            centreline of the landing gear when it is extended and the
            leading edge of the front wheels,

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} a drop deck,

          - [(C)]{.lawlabel} a mounted, pull-out platform,

          - [(D)]{.lawlabel} one or more steps for side-door access,

          - [(E)]{.lawlabel} a lift gate; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a short refrigerated box van trailer that is
        less than 10.67 metres (35 feet) in length and has at least one
        of the side features listed in clauses (a)(ii)(A) to
        (E). ([remorque fourgon non aérodynamique]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[non-box trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[non-box trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a tank trailer that
      is designed to transport liquids or gases, a trailer that is
      designed to carry a temporarily mounted shipping container or a
      trailer that is designed to accommodate side-loading cargo onto a
      single, continuous load-bearing surface that runs from the rear of
      the trailer to at least the trailer's kingpin and that may have
      curtains, straps or other devices to restrain or protect the cargo
      during transport, including side walls that do not completely
      enclose the cargo space. ([remorque sans fourgon]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[N~2~O]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[N~2~O]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means nitrous
      oxide. ([N~2~O]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[on-road vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[on-road vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a self-propelled
      vehicle that is designed for or capable of transporting persons,
      property, material or permanently or temporarily affixed apparatus
      on a highway, but does not mean a vehicle that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cannot exceed a speed of 40 km/h (25 miles per
        hour) on a level paved surface;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} lacks features customarily associated with safe
        and practical highway use such as a reverse gear, a differential
        or safety features that are required by federal or provincial
        laws;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} exhibits features that render its use on a
        highway unsafe, impractical or highly unlikely, such as tracked
        road contact means or inordinate size; or

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is a military vehicle that is designed for use
        in combat or combat support. ([véhicule
        routier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[partial-aero]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[partial-aero]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a box van
      trailer other than a non-aero box van trailer, means that the
      trailer

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has at least one of the following side
        features:

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a belly box that occupies at least half of
          the length of both sides of the trailer between the centreline
          of the landing gear when it is extended and the leading edge
          of the front wheels,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a drop deck,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a mounted, pull-out platform,

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} one or more steps for side-door access,

        - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a lift gate; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a long dry box van trailer or a
        long refrigerated box van trailer, has at least one of the
        following rear features:

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a hinged loading ramp,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a lift gate,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a mount for a forklift,

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} for the 2023 model year or an earlier model
          year, a roll-up door. ([partiellement
          aérodynamique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[plug-in hybrid vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[plug-in hybrid vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a hybrid
      vehicle that has an energy storage system that can be recharged
      from an electric source that is not on board the
      vehicle. ([véhicule hybride rechargeable]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[power take-off]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[power take-off]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a secondary engine
      shaft or other system of a vehicle that provides substantial
      auxiliary power for purposes unrelated to vehicle propulsion or
      the functioning of customary vehicle accessories such as air
      conditioning, power steering and basic accessories. ([prise de
      mouvement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[refrigerated box van trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[refrigerated box van trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a box
      van trailer that has a self-contained heating, ventilation or
      air-conditioning system. ([remorque fourgon
      frigorifique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[regional vocational vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[regional vocational vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      vocational vehicle that has any of the characteristics of a
      vehicle to which the regional duty cycle is applicable under
      section 510 of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1037,
      subpart F, of the CFR. ([véhicule spécialisé
      régional]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[short]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[short]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a trailer, means not
      more than 15.24 metres (50 feet) in
      length. ([courte]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[sleeper cab]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sleeper cab]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a tractor cab that has a
      compartment located behind the driver's seat that is designed to
      be used as a sleeping accommodation and that is accessible either
      from the driver's compartment or from outside the
      vehicle. ([cabine couchette]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[spark-ignition engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[spark-ignition engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an engine that
      operates under characteristics significantly similar to the
      theoretical Otto combustion cycle and uses a spark plug or other
      sparking device. ([moteur à allumage commandé]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[static loaded radius]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[static loaded radius]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the distance
      between the level surface where the vehicle is located and the
      axle centre measured at curb weight when the vehicle is
      stationary, with the wheels parallel to the vehicle's longitudinal
      centre line and the tires inflated to the manufacturer's
      recommended cold tire inflation pressure. ([rayon sous charge
      statique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[steady state duty cycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[steady state duty cycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the test
      cycle that is referred to in section 1362 of Title 40, chapter I,
      subchapter C, part 86, subpart N, of the CFR. ([cycle de service
      permanent]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[test group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[test group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a company's
      Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty vehicles, excluding those that are
      vocational vehicles or incomplete vocational vehicles, and the
      company's engines referred to in section 25, means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if they are covered by an EPA certificate, the
        grouping for which the EPA certificate was issued; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if they are not covered by an EPA certificate,
        the grouping determined in accordance with section 1827 of Title
        40, chapter I, subchapter C, part 86, subpart S, of the
        CFR. ([groupe d'essai]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[test weight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[test weight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the vehicle weight that
      is used or represented during testing. ([masse à
      l'essai]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[tire pressure monitoring system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tire pressure monitoring system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      system that is installed on a vehicle or a trailer to monitor the
      air pressure in each tire and alert the driver when the tire
      pressure falls below a specified value. ([système de surveillance
      de la pression des pneus]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[tire rolling resistance level]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tire rolling resistance level]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      rolling resistance of a tire configuration, expressed in kilograms
      per tonne. ([niveau de résistance au roulement du
      pneu]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[tractor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tractor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Class 7 or Class 8
      heavy-duty vehicle that is manufactured primarily for pulling a
      trailer but not for carrying cargo other than cargo in the
      trailer. ([tracteur routier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means equipment with wheels that
      is designed to carry cargo and be pulled by a tractor when coupled
      to the tractor's fifth wheel, excluding equipment that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is less than 10.67 metres (35 feet) in length
        and has three axles;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has four or more axles;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has adjacent axles whose centrelines are at
        least 305 cm (120 inches) apart and, if the distance between the
        axles is adjustable, cannot be set closer together;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is intended to be used as a temporary or
        permanent residence or as an office or other work space; or

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} is designed for use in military combat or
        combat support. ([remorque]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[trailer family]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trailer family]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a company's
      trailers, means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if they are covered by an EPA certificate, the
        grouping for which the EPA certificate was issued; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if they are not covered by an EPA certificate,
        the trailers within a grouping referred to in section 230 of
        Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1037, subpart C, of the
        CFR, except that a reference to "vehicle" in sections 230(b) and
        (d)(3) of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1037, subpart
        C, of the CFR must be read as a reference to
        "trailer". ([famille de remorques]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[transient duty cycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[transient duty cycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the test cycle
      that is referred to in section 1333 of Title 40, chapter I,
      subchapter C, part 86, subpart N, of the CFR. ([cycle de service
      transitoire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[urban vocational vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[urban vocational vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      vocational vehicle that has any of the characteristics of a
      vehicle to which the urban duty cycle is applicable under section
      510 of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1037, subpart F, of
      the CFR. ([véhicule spécialisé urbain]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[vehicle configuration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vehicle configuration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of
      Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty vehicles and cab-complete
      vehicles, a [[configuration]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in
      section 1819(d)(12)(i) of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter C, part
      86, subpart S, of the CFR. ([configuration de
      véhicule]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[vehicle family]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vehicle family]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a company's
      tractors or vocational vehicles, means 

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if they are covered by an EPA certificate, the
        grouping for which the EPA certificate was issued; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if they are not covered by an EPA certificate,
        the grouping determined in accordance with section 230 of Title
        40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1037, subpart C, of the
        CFR. ([famille de véhicules]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[vehicle service class]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vehicle service class]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any one of the
      following groups:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} light heavy-duty vehicles;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} medium heavy-duty vehicles; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} heavy heavy-duty vehicles. ([classe de
        service]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[vehicle subconfiguration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vehicle subconfiguration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, within a
      vehicle configuration of Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty vehicles
      and cab-complete vehicles, a unique combination of equivalent test
      weight and road load horsepower, and any other operational
      characteristics or parameters that may significantly affect CO~2~
      emissions within the vehicle configuration. ([sous-configuration
      de véhicule]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[vocational tractor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vocational tractor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any of the
      following tractors that are not designed primarily to operate at
      high and constant speeds such as on highways, or that would not
      benefit from efficiency improvements designed for line-haul
      tractors:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a low-roof tractor that is designed for local
        pickup and delivery;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a tractor that is designed for both on-road and
        off-road use, such as a tractor with a reinforced frame and
        increased ground clearance; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a tractor of the 2020 model year or an earlier
        model year that has a GCWR of 54 431 kg (120,000 pounds) or
        more. ([tracteur routier spécialisé]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[vocational vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vocational vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any of the
      following:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a Class 4, Class 5 or Class 6 heavy-duty
        vehicle;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a Class 7 or Class 8 heavy-duty vehicle that is
        not a tractor;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a vocational tractor;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a heavy-duty incomplete vehicle that is not a
        cab-complete vehicle and is equipped with an engine conforming
        to the alternative standard referred to in section 25; or

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a Class 2B or Class 3 heavy-duty vehicle that
        is not subject to section 1819 of Title 40, chapter I,
        subchapter C, part 86, subpart S, of the CFR. ([véhicule
        spécialisé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}CFR

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Standards that are incorporated by reference in these
  Regulations from the CFR are those expressly set out in the CFR and
  must be read as excluding

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} references to the EPA or the Administrator of the
    EPA exercising discretion in any way;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} references to the Secretary of Transportation
    exercising discretion in any way;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} alternative standards related to fleet averages,
    other averages, emission credits, small volume manufacturers or
    financial hardship; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} standards or evidence of conformity of any
    authority other than the EPA.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), a reference in
  the CFR to "carbon-related exhaust emissions" and "CREE" must be read
  as "CO~2~ emissions".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The calculations and measurements in these
  Regulations must be rounded in accordance with section 20(e) of Title
  40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1065, subpart A, of the CFR, unless
  otherwise provided in

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} these Regulations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} part 1037 of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, of
    the CFR, for the applicable standards and test procedures in the
    case of trailers and heavy-duty vehicles other than those referred
    to in paragraph (b.1);

  - [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} section 1819 of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter
    C, part 86, subpart S, of the CFR, for the applicable standards and
    test procedures in the case of Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty
    vehicles and cab-complete vehicles, excluding those that are
    vocational vehicles or incomplete vocational vehicles; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} part 1036 of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, of
    the CFR, for the applicable standards and test procedures in the
    case of heavy-duty engines.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Useful life

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise provided in these Regulations,
  [[useful life]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} refers to the period of time or
  use in respect of which an emission standard applies to, as the case
  may be,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty vehicles and
    cab-complete vehicles --- excluding those that are vocational
    vehicles or incomplete vocational vehicles --- namely,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the 2020 model year and earlier model years,
      11 years or 193 121 km (120,000 miles), whichever occurs first,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for the 2021 model year and subsequent model
      years, 15 years or 241 401 km (150,000 miles), whichever occurs
      first;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} vocational vehicles that are light heavy-duty
    vehicles and incomplete vocational vehicles that are to become light
    heavy-duty vehicles, namely,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the 2020 model year and earlier model years,
      10 years or 177 027 km (110,000 miles), whichever occurs first,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for the 2021 model year and subsequent model
      years, 15 years or 241 401 km (150,000 miles), whichever occurs
      first;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} vocational vehicles that are medium heavy-duty
    vehicles, incomplete vocational vehicles that are to become medium
    heavy-duty vehicles, Class 7 tractors and incomplete tractors that
    are to become Class 7 tractors, namely, 10 years or 297 728 km
    (185,000 miles), whichever occurs first;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} vocational vehicles that are heavy heavy-duty
    vehicles, incomplete vocational vehicles that are to become heavy
    heavy-duty vehicles, Class 8 tractors and incomplete tractors that
    are to become Class 8 tractors, namely, 10 years or 700 064 km
    (435,000 miles), whichever occurs first;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} heavy-duty engines, namely,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of spark-ignition engines and of
      light heavy-duty engines that are compression-ignition engines of
      the 2021 model year or a subsequent model year, 15 years or
      241 401 km (150,000 miles), whichever occurs first, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the same useful life as that
      which is set out for the type of engine in question in section 2
      of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter C, part 86, subpart A, of the
      CFR for emissions of oxides of nitrogen (NO~X~), hydrocarbon (HC),
      particulate matter (PM) and carbon monoxide (CO); and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} trailers, namely, 10 years.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Roof height --- tractors

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (7) and (8), "roof height"
  refers to the maximum height of a tractor, rounded to the nearest
  inch, excluding small accessories such as exhaust pipes and antennas,
  but including large accessories such as roof fairings, and measured
  with tires inflated to the manufacturer's recommended cold tire
  inflation pressure and without occupants or cargo onboard.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Roof height measurement --- tractors

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The roof height of a tractor must be measured with a
  static loaded radius equal to the arithmetic mean of the largest and
  smallest static loaded radius of the tires that are recommended for
  the tractor by the manufacturer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjustable roof fairing --- tractors

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a tractor equipped with an adjustable
  roof fairing, the roof height must be measured with the fairing in its
  lowest setting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Length --- trailer

  [(8.1)]{.lawlabel} The length of a trailer corresponds to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the outer dimensions of its load-carrying
    structure, excluding any aerodynamic devices and any heating,
    ventilating or air-conditioning systems; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a B-train trailer, the outer
    dimensions of its load-carrying structure, excluding its rear
    fifth-wheel coupling section, any aerodynamic devices and any
    heating, ventilating or air-conditioning systems.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Number of axles

  [(8.2)]{.lawlabel} The number of axles on a vehicle or trailer
  includes any axles that can be used to carry the loaded or unloaded
  weight of the vehicle or the trailer while in motion, including lift
  axles.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Family emission limit

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} A family emission limit and a CO~2~ family
  certification level must be expressed to the same number of decimal
  places as the emission standard they replace.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Spark-ignition engines

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of these Regulations, a
  spark-ignition engine of the 2020 model year or an earlier model year
  that is regulated as a diesel engine under part 86 of Title 40,
  chapter I, subchapter C, of the CFR must conform to the standards,
  test procedures and calculation methods applicable to a
  compression-ignition engine of the same model year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compression-ignition engines

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of these Regulations, a
  compression-ignition engine of the 2020 model year or an earlier model
  year that is regulated as an Otto-cycle engine under part 86 of Title
  40, chapter I, subchapter C, of the CFR must conform to the standards,
  test procedures and calculation methods applicable to a spark-ignition
  engine of the same model year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Various engines

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of these Regulations, the following
  engines must conform to the standards, test procedures and calculation
  methods applicable to a compression-ignition engine of the same model
  year:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a gas turbine heavy-duty engine; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a heavy-duty engine that is not a spark-ignition
    engine or compression-ignition engine.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 1
- [SOR/2022-204, s. 3](#1375969-1377406){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1375969-1377406.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-1-20181116.html "Link to previous version of section 1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Concurrent sale

**[[1.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
these Regulations, a vehicle, engine or trailer of a given model year
that is sold in Canada is considered to be sold concurrently in Canada
and in the United States if a vehicle, engine or trailer of that model
year that belongs to the same test group, vehicle family, engine family
or trailer family is offered for sale in the United States during the
365 days preceding

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle, engine or trailer that is
  imported into Canada, the day on which it is imported; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle, engine or trailer that is
  manufactured in Canada,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the national emissions mark is
    applied to the vehicle, engine or trailer, if known, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the day referred to in subparagraph (i) is not
    known, the day on which the main assembly of the vehicle or the
    manufacture of the engine or the trailer, as the case may be, is
    completed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-186, s. 61
- SOR/2018-98, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-1.1-20150716.html "Link to previous version of section 1.1"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-800723 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed vehicles, engines and
equipment

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations apply to
the vehicles, engines and equipment that are prescribed by subsections
5(1) to (3).

## [Model Year]{.HTitleText1} {#h-800729 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Model year

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsection (3), a year that is used by a manufacturer as a model
  year must,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the period of production of a model of
    heavy-duty vehicle, heavy-duty engine or trailer does not include
    January 1 of a calendar year, correspond to the calendar year during
    which the period of production falls; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the period of production of a model of
    heavy-duty vehicle, heavy-duty engine or trailer includes January 1
    of a calendar year, correspond to that calendar year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of production

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The period of production of a model of heavy-duty
  vehicle, heavy-duty engine or trailer must include only one January 1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vocational vehicles and tractors --- as
  of 2021

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For vocational vehicles, incomplete vocational
  vehicles, tractors and incomplete tractors whose main assembly is
  completed on or after January 1, 2021, the vehicle's model year is the
  calendar year corresponding to the calendar year during which its main
  assembly is completed. However, a company may choose to designate the
  vehicle's model year as being

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the model year that corresponds to the calendar
    year after the calendar year during which the vehicle's main
    assembly is completed; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the model year that corresponds to the calendar
    year before the calendar year during which the vehicle's main
    assembly is completed if,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle whose engine is
      installed in the United States, the engine's model year is also
      from an earlier year in accordance with section 601(a)(2) of Title
      40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1037, subpart G, of the CFR and,
      if the engine is installed after March 31 of the calendar year
      during which the vehicle's main assembly is completed, the company
      has obtained prior approval from the EPA, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle whose engine is
      installed in Canada,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the engine's model year is also from an earlier
        year and the standards applicable under these Regulations to
        engines of that earlier model year are the same as those
        applicable to engines of a model year that corresponds to the
        calendar year during which the vehicle's main assembly is
        completed, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the engine's model year is the model year that
        corresponds to the calendar year before the calendar year during
        which the vehicle's main assembly is completed and the engine is
        installed before March 31 of the calendar year during which the
        vehicle's main assembly is completed.

::: HistoricalNote
