## [Prescribed Classes of Vehicles, Engines and Equipment]{.HTitleText1} {#h-800745 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2018-98, s. 4

\]
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Heavy-duty vehicles

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following classes of vehicles are prescribed for the purposes of the
  definition [[vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 149 of the
  Act:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty vehicles;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} vocational vehicles;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} tractors; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} heavy-duty incomplete vehicles.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Heavy-duty engines

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Heavy-duty engines are prescribed for the purposes of
  the definition [[engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 149 of the
  Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Trailers

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Box van trailers and non-box trailers are
  prescribed for the purposes of the definition
  [[equipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 149 of the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusion

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The prescribed classes of vehicles, engines and
  equipment set out in subsections (1), (2) and (2.1), respectively, do
  not include heavy-duty vehicles, heavy-duty engines or trailers that
  are to be exported and that are accompanied by written evidence
  establishing that they will not be sold for use or used in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transportation within Canada ---
  heavy-duty vehicles

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of section 152 of the Act, the
  prescribed vehicles are the vehicles referred to in subsection (1) for
  which the main assembly is completed in Canada, other than a vehicle
  that will be used in Canada solely for purposes of exhibition,
  demonstration, evaluation or testing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transportation within Canada ---
  heavy-duty engines

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of section 152 of the Act, the
  prescribed engines are the engines referred to in subsection (2) that
  are manufactured in Canada, other than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an engine that will be used in Canada solely for
    purposes of exhibition, demonstration, evaluation or testing;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an engine that is to be installed in a heavy-duty
    vehicle before sale to the vehicle's first retail purchaser; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an engine that is to be installed as a replacement
    engine in a heavy-duty vehicle that has a national emissions mark
    applied to it, if the replacement engine is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} of the same model year as the original engine,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} identical to or better than the original engine
      with respect to emissions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transportation within Canada ---
  trailers

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of section 152 of the Act, the
  prescribed equipment is box van trailers and non-box trailers that are
  manufactured in Canada, other than trailers that will be used in
  Canada solely for purposes of exhibition, demonstration, evaluation or
  testing.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 5
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-5-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 5"){.PITLink}
:::

## [National Emissions Mark]{.HTitleText1} {#h-800774 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company that intends to apply a national emissions mark to a vehicle,
  engine or trailer must apply to the Minister for authorization.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of application

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The application must be signed by a person who is
  authorized to act on behalf of the company and must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and street address of the company's head
    office and, if different, its mailing address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the classes of vehicles, engines or trailers for
    which the authorization is requested;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the street address of the location at which the
    national emissions mark will be applied to the vehicles, engines or
    trailers;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the street address where the records referred to in
    section 59 will be maintained; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} information demonstrating that the company is
    capable of verifying compliance with the standards set out in these
    Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a company that, on
  the day on which these Regulations come into force, is authorized to
  apply the national emissions mark to a vehicle or engine under the
  On-Road Vehicle and Engine Emission Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 6
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 6"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}National emissions mark

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  national emissions mark is the mark set out in the schedule.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dimensions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The national emissions mark must be at least 7 mm in
  height and 10 mm in width.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Location

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 11(2), the national emissions
  mark must be displayed

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on or immediately beside the U.S. emission control
    information label or U.S. engine information label referred to in
    paragraph 53(d);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on or immediately beside the compliance label
    applied in accordance with the [Motor Vehicle Safety
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1038); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} on or immediately beside the compliance label
    referred to in section 8, 9 or 9.1, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The national emissions mark must be displayed on a
  label that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is permanently applied to the vehicle, engine or
    trailer;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is resistant to or protected against any weather
    condition; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} bears inscriptions that are legible and indelible
    and that are indented, embossed or in a colour that contrasts with
    the background of the label.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization number

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (6), a company that has been
  authorized to apply the national emissions mark must display the
  authorization number assigned by the Minister in figures that are at
  least 2 mm in height, immediately below or to the right of the
  national emissions mark.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A company is not required to display its
  authorization number on a vehicle or trailer if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the company is authorized by the Minister of
    Transport to apply the national safety mark in accordance with the
    [Motor Vehicle Safety Act](/eng/acts/M-10.01);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the company applies both the national emissions
    mark and the national safety mark to the vehicle or the trailer; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the national emissions mark is displayed on the
    same label as the national safety mark.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 7
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-7-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 7"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Labelling]{.HTitleText1} {#h-800813 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non EPA-certified engines

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Heavy-duty engines and the engines
  referred to in section 25 that are imported or manufactured in Canada
  --- other than EPA-certified engines that bear the label referred to
  in subparagraph 53(d)(ii) --- must bear a compliance label that sets
  out the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), the statement "THIS
    ENGINE CONFORMS TO ALL APPLICABLE STANDARDS PRESCRIBED BY THE
    CANADIAN REGULATIONS ENTITLED Heavy-duty Vehicle and Engine
    Greenhouse Gas Emission Regulations IN EFFECT FOR MODEL YEAR
    \[*insert model year*\] / [CE MOTEUR EST CONFORME AUX NORMES QUI LUI
    SONT APPLICABLES PRÉVUES AU RÈGLEMENT CANADIEN INTITULÉ [Règlement
    sur les émissions de gaz à effet de serre des véhicules lourds et de
    leurs moteurs](/eng/regulations/DORS-2013-24) EN VIGUEUR POUR
    L'ANNÉE DE MODÈLE \[*inscrire l'année de modèle*\]]{.otherLang
    lang="fr"}";

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the engine's manufacturer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the engine's model year if a national emissions
    mark is applied to the engine;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (3), the engine's date of
    manufacture;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (3), the engine's unique
    identification number;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the model designations;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the engine displacement;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the identification of the emission control system;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the engine family's or test group's identification
    code, as the case may be;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the limits on the types of use for the engine to
    ensure that the emission standards set out in these Regulations are
    complied with;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} the engine specifications and adjustments
    recommended by the engine's manufacturer;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a spark-ignition engine, the valve
    lash, idle speed, ignition timing and idle air-fuel mixture setting
    procedure and value;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a compression-ignition engine, the
    engine power specified by the manufacturer and expressed in HP, the
    RPM at the specified horsepower, the fuel rate at the specified
    horsepower expressed in mm^3^ per stroke, the valve lash, idle speed
    and initial injection timing; and

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a heavy-duty engine that is exempted
    under subsection 17(1.1), the engine's model year and a statement,
    in both official languages, that the engine is exempted under that
    subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}National emissions mark

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(a) does not apply when a national
  emissions mark is applied to the engine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of manufacture and unique
  identification number

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The engine's date of manufacture referred to in
  paragraph (1)(d) and unique identification number referred to in
  paragraph (1)(e) may, instead of being set out on the label, be
  permanently affixed, engraved or stamped on the engine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Engines referred to in section 25

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In the case of spark-ignition engines referred to in
  section 25, the label referred to in subsection (1) must also set out
  one of the following statements, whichever applies:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement in both official languages that the
    engine conforms to the alternative greenhouse gas emission standards
    for engines of Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty vehicles; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the statement referred to in section 1819(k)(8)(v)
    of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter C, part 86, subpart S, of the
    CFR.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Engines referred to in subsection 31(1)

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} In the case of compression-ignition engines referred
  to in subsection 31(1), the label referred to in subsection (1) must
  also set out one of the following statements, whichever applies:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement in both official languages that the
    engine conforms to the alternative CO~2~ emission standard based on
    model year 2011 compression-ignition engines; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the statement referred to in section 620(d) of
    Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1036, subpart G, of the CFR.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-186, s. 62
- SOR/2018-98, s. 8
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-8-20150716.html "Link to previous version of section 8"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non EPA-certified vehicles

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Heavy-duty vehicles that are
  imported or manufactured in Canada --- other than EPA-certified
  heavy-duty vehicles that bear the label referred to in subparagraph
  53(d)(i) --- must bear a compliance label that sets out the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), the statement "THIS
    VEHICLE CONFORMS TO ALL APPLICABLE STANDARDS PRESCRIBED BY THE
    CANADIAN REGULATIONS ENTITLED Heavy-duty Vehicle and Engine
    Greenhouse Gas Emission Regulations IN EFFECT FOR MODEL YEAR
    \[*insert model year*\] / [CE VÉHICULE EST CONFORME AUX NORMES QUI
    LUI SONT APPLICABLES PRÉVUES AU RÈGLEMENT CANADIEN INTITULÉ
    [Règlement sur les émissions de gaz à effet de serre des véhicules
    lourds et de leurs moteurs](/eng/regulations/DORS-2013-24) EN
    VIGUEUR POUR L'ANNÉE DE MODÈLE \[*inscrire l'année de
    modèle*\]]{.otherLang lang="fr"}";

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the vehicle's manufacturer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle's model year if a national emissions
    mark is applied to the vehicle;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (3), the date on which the
    vehicle's main assembly is completed;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the type of vehicle, in both official languages,
    referred to in paragraph 18(3)(a);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle family's or test group's identification
    code, as the case may be;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} except in the case of a vocational vehicle,
    incomplete vocational vehicle, tractor or incomplete tractor of the
    2021 model year or a subsequent model year, the identification of
    the vehicle's emission control system;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vocational vehicle or incomplete
    vocational vehicle referred to in subsection 26(3), the vehicle's
    model year and a statement, in both official languages, that the
    vehicle is exempted under that subsection;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vocational tractor or vocational
    tractor that is an incomplete tractor, a statement, in both official
    languages, that the vehicle is a vocational tractor;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vocational vehicle, incomplete
    vocational vehicle, tractor or incomplete tractor that is exempted
    under subsection 17(1), the vehicle's model year and a statement, in
    both official languages, that the vehicle is exempted under that
    subsection;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a Class 2B or Class 3 heavy-duty
    vehicle or cab-complete vehicle --- excluding those that are
    vocational vehicles or incomplete vocational vehicles --- the engine
    displacement and the CO~2~ emission value determined by variable A
    in accordance with subsection 23(1) for that vehicle configuration
    and if applicable, the N~2~O and CH~4~ family emission limits;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vocational vehicle, incomplete
    vocational vehicle, tractor or incomplete tractor referred to in
    subsection 12.2(2), a statement, in both official languages, that
    the vehicle is equipped with an engine that meets the conditions set
    out in that subsection 12.2(2) and conforms to the standards
    referred to in subsection 12.2(3);

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a hybrid vehicle referred to in
    subsection 12.2(4), a statement, in both official languages, that
    the vehicle is equipped with an engine that conforms to the
    applicable alternative standards referred to in that subsection; and

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vocational vehicle or incomplete
    vocational vehicle in respect of which the company makes an election
    referred to in subsection 26(1.2), a statement to that effect, in
    both official languages, and the type of vocational vehicle or
    incomplete vocational vehicle referred to in column 1 of the table
    to that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}National emissions mark

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(a) does not apply if a national
  emissions mark is applied to the vehicle or if the information
  referred to in paragraph (1)(h) or (j) is set out on the label.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date on which main assembly is
  completed

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The date referred to in paragraph (1)(d) may, instead
  of being set out on the label, be permanently affixed, engraved or
  stamped on the vehicle.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-186, s. 63
- SOR/2018-98, ss. 9, 60
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-9-20150716.html "Link to previous version of section 9"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Trailers not certified by EPA

- **[[9.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every box van trailer and non-box
  trailer that is imported or manufactured in Canada --- other than
  EPA-certified trailers that bear the U.S. emission control information
  label referred to in subparagraph 53(d)(i) --- must bear a compliance
  label that sets out the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), the statement "THIS
    TRAILER CONFORMS TO ALL APPLICABLE STANDARDS PRESCRIBED BY THE
    CANADIAN REGULATIONS ENTITLED Heavy-duty Vehicle and Engine
    Greenhouse Gas Emission Regulations IN EFFECT FOR MODEL YEAR
    \[*insert model year*\] / [CETTE REMORQUE EST CONFORME AUX NORMES
    QUI LUI SONT APPLICABLES PRÉVUES AU RÈGLEMENT CANADIEN INTITULÉ
    [Règlement sur les émissions de gaz à effet de serre des véhicules
    lourds et de leurs moteurs](/eng/regulations/DORS-2013-24) EN
    VIGUEUR POUR L'ANNÉE DE MODÈLE \[*inscrire l'année de
    modèle*\]]{.otherLang lang="fr"}";

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the trailer's manufacturer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the trailer's model year if a national emissions
    mark is applied to it;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (3), the trailer's date of
    manufacture;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the type of trailer, in both official languages;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the trailer family's identification code; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a trailer that is exempted under
    section 17.1 or 17.2, a statement in both official languages that
    the trailer is exempted under that section and, if not already
    indicated under paragraph (c), the trailer's model year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}National emissions mark

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(a) does not apply if a national
  emissions mark is applied to the trailer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of manufacture

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The trailer's date of manufacture may, instead of
  being set out on the label, be permanently affixed, engraved or
  stamped on the trailer.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements

**[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** Any label that is
required by these Regulations, other than a U.S. emission control
information label or U.S. engine information label referred to in
paragraph 53(d), must

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} be applied to a conspicuous and readily accessible
  location;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} be permanently applied to the vehicle or the trailer
  and, in the case of an engine, be permanently applied to an engine
  part that is necessary for normal engine operation and does not
  normally require replacement during the engine's useful life;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} be resistant to or protected against any weather
  condition;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} have lettering that is

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} clear and indelible,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} indented, embossed or in a colour that contrasts
    with the background colour of the label, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in block capitals and numerals that are not less
    than 2 mm in height; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} have units that are identified by the appropriate
  name or symbol.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 11
:::

[Previous
Version](section-10-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 10"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Vehicles Manufactured in Stages]{.HTitleText1} {#h-800896 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  company alters a heavy-duty vehicle or heavy-duty incomplete vehicle
  that was in conformity with these Regulations in such a manner that
  its stated type of vehicle referred to in paragraph 18(3)(a) is no
  longer accurate, or if the company alters the emission control system,
  alters an engine configuration in a way that might affect emissions or
  replaces any of the components of the vehicle that might alter the
  value of a parameter used in the GEM computer simulation model, the
  company must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the U.S. emission control information
    label referred to in subparagraph 53(d)(i), the compliance label
    referred to in section 9 and the national emissions mark, as the
    case may be, remain applied to the altered vehicle;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the vehicle, once altered, conforms to
    all applicable standards;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} apply to the altered vehicle an additional label
    that sets out the following information:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the words "THIS VEHICLE WAS ALTERED BY / [CE
      VÉHICULE A ÉTÉ MODIFIÉ PAR]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", followed by
      the name of the company that altered the vehicle,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the month and year during which the alteration
      was made to the vehicle,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the national emissions mark, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the type of vehicle referred to in paragraph
      18(3)(a), if it differs from the type set out on the compliance
      label referred to in section 9 or if the regulatory subcategory
      that is set out on the U.S. emission control information label
      referred to in subparagraph 53(d)(i) is changed, as the case may
      be; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} obtain and produce the evidence of conformity
    referred to in section 54 for the altered vehicle in a form and
    manner that is satisfactory to the Minister before the vehicle
    leaves its possession or control.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}National emissions mark

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The national emissions mark referred to in
  subparagraph (1)(c)(iii) must be displayed on a label applied to the
  vehicle immediately beside any previously applied label on which the
  national emissions mark appears or immediately beside the U.S.
  emission control information label referred to in subparagraph
  53(d)(i).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-participation in credit system

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company that alters a vehicle in accordance with
  this section must not participate in the CO~2~ emission credit system
  set out in sections 34 to 47 with respect to that altered vehicle.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 12
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Greenhouse Gas Emission Standards]{.HTitleText1} {#h-800914 .Part}

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-800915 .Subheading}

#### [Application]{.HTitleText3} {#h-800916 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2018-98, s. 13

\]
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}January 1, 2014

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), these
  Regulations apply to vehicles for which the main assembly is completed
  on or after January 1, 2014.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Election

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company may elect to comply with these Regulations
  with respect to its heavy-duty vehicles of the 2014 model year for
  which the main assembly is completed before January 1, 2014 for the
  purpose of participation in the CO~2~ emission credit system set out
  in sections 34 to 47.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}January 1, 2020

**[[12.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.1 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations
apply to trailers whose manufacture is completed on or after January 1,
2020.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 14
:::

#### [Engine Installed in Heavy-duty Vehicle]{.HTitleText3} {#h-800928 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vocational vehicles or tractors

- **[[12.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (4),
  every vocational vehicle, incomplete vocational vehicle, tractor and
  incomplete tractor whose main assembly is completed after the coming
  into force of this section must be equipped with a heavy-duty engine
  that conforms to the following standards:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the standards set out in the On-Road Vehicle and
    Engine Emission Regulations that are applicable to [[heavy-duty
    engines]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 1(1) of
    those Regulations that are of the following model year:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle whose main assembly is
      completed before January 1, 2021, the model year of the engine
      with which the vocational vehicle, incomplete vocational vehicle,
      tractor or incomplete tractor is equipped, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle whose main assembly is
      completed on or after January 1, 2021, the model year that
      corresponds to

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the calendar year during which the main
        assembly of the vocational vehicle, incomplete vocational
        vehicle, tractor or incomplete tractor in which the engine is
        installed is completed,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the calendar year after the calendar year
        during which the main assembly of the vocational vehicle,
        incomplete vocational vehicle, tractor or incomplete tractor in
        which the engine is installed is completed, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} if the conditions set out in subparagraph
        4(3)(b)(i) or clause 4(3)(b)(ii)(A) or (B) are met, the calendar
        year before the calendar year during which the main assembly of
        the vocational vehicle, incomplete vocational vehicle, tractor
        or incomplete tractor in which the engine is installed is
        completed; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the standards set out in these Regulations that are
    applicable to engines that are of the following model year:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle whose main assembly is
      completed before January 1, 2021, the model year of the engine
      with which the vocational vehicle, incomplete vocational vehicle,
      tractor or incomplete tractor is equipped, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle whose main assembly is
      completed on or after January 1, 2021, the model year that
      corresponds to

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the calendar year during which the main
        assembly of the vocational vehicle, incomplete vocational
        vehicle, tractor or incomplete tractor in which the engine is
        installed is completed,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the calendar year after the calendar year
        during which the main assembly of the vocational vehicle,
        incomplete vocational vehicle, tractor or incomplete tractor in
        which the engine is installed is completed, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} if the conditions set out in subparagraph
        4(3)(b)(i) or clause 4(3)(b)(ii)(A) or (B) are met, the calendar
        year before the calendar year during which the main assembly of
        the vocational vehicle, incomplete vocational vehicle, tractor
        or incomplete tractor in which the engine is installed is
        completed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Election

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company may elect not to comply with subsection (1)
  for a vocational vehicle, incomplete vocational vehicle, tractor or
  incomplete tractor if the company reports this election in its end of
  model year report in accordance with section 48 and the vehicle's
  engine has previously been sold to a first retail purchaser and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has not reached the end of its useful life since
    its original date of manufacture;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has accumulated less than 160 935 km (100,000
    miles) of operation since its original date of manufacture;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} less than three years have passed since its
    original date of manufacture; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is of the 2010 model year or a subsequent model
    year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Standards --- engine installed in
  vehicle

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The engine referred to in subsection (2) must conform
  to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the standards set out in the On-Road Vehicle and
    Engine Emission Regulations that are applicable to [[heavy-duty
    engines]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 1(1) of
    those Regulations that are of the model year corresponding to the
    engine's original date of manufacture; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the standards set out in these Regulations that are
    applicable to engines that are of the model year corresponding to
    the engine's original date of manufacture.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hybrid vehicles --- alternate engine
  standards

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In the case of vocational vehicles, incomplete
  vocational vehicles, tractors and incomplete tractors that are or are
  to become hybrid vehicles whose engine provides energy to the
  vehicle's energy storage features, if there are no engines on the
  market that conform to the standards referred to in subsection (1) and
  that have the physical or performance characteristics necessary for
  the operation of the vehicle, a company may, for any given model year
  up to and including the 2027 model year, elect to equip up to 100 such
  vehicles with a heavy-duty engine that, instead of conforming to the
  standards referred to in that subsection, conforms to the alternative
  standards set out in section 10(g) or 11(g), as the case may be, of
  Title 40, chapter I, subchapter C, part 86, subpart A, of the CFR.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 14
- [SOR/2022-204, s. 5](#1375980-1377422){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1375980-1377422.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-12.2-20181116.html "Link to previous version of section 12.2"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Heavy-duty Vehicles, Heavy-duty Engines and Trailers Covered by EPA Certificate]{.HTitleText3} {#h-800956 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conforming to EPA certificate

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (4), (7.1)
  and (8), a heavy-duty vehicle, heavy-duty engine or trailer of a given
  model year that is covered by an EPA certificate and bears the U.S.
  emission control information label or U.S. engine information label
  referred to in paragraph 53(d) must conform to the certification and
  in-use standards referred to in the EPA certificate instead of to the
  following standards, whichever apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} sections 14 to 16 and subsection 20(1) for Class 2B
    and Class 3 heavy-duty vehicles and cab-complete vehicles, excluding
    those that are vocational vehicles or incomplete vocational
    vehicles;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sections 14 to 16 and, as the case may be,
    subsection 26(1), (1.1), (1.2) or (1.3) for vocational vehicles and
    incomplete vocational vehicles;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} sections 14 to 16 and, as the case may be,
    subsection 27(1) or paragraph 27(1.1)(a) for tractors and incomplete
    tractors;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} sections 14 and 15 and subsection 29(1) and, as the
    case may be, section 30 or subsection 31(1), (2) or (5) for
    heavy-duty engines; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} section 15 and, as the case may be, subsection
    16.1(1) or 33.1(1) or (2) for trailers.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceeding N~2~O or CH~4~ emission
  standard --- vehicles

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 20(3) to (6) apply in respect of a
  company's Class 2B or Class 3 heavy-duty vehicles or cab-complete
  vehicles --- excluding those that are vocational vehicles or
  incomplete vocational vehicles --- that are covered by an EPA
  certificate and, as the case may be, conform to a N~2~O or CH~4~
  family emission limit that exceeds the N~2~O or CH~4~ emission
  standard applicable to their model year under these Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Comply with CO~2~ emission credit
  system

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), when a company participates
  in the CO~2~ emission credit system set out in sections 34 to 47 for
  its heavy-duty vehicles or heavy-duty engines that are covered by an
  EPA certificate, it must comply with the CO~2~ emission credit system
  provisions that relate to the emission standards referred to in
  subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fleets --- vehicles

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A company that manufactures or imports a vocational
  vehicle, incomplete vocational vehicle, tractor or incomplete tractor
  that is covered by an EPA certificate and conforms to a CO~2~ family
  emission limit that exceeds the CO~2~ emission standard applicable to
  the model year of that vehicle under these Regulations, must
  participate in the CO~2~ emission credit system set out in sections 34
  to 47 and must, in accordance with section 18, group into fleets

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least 50% of its vocational vehicles and
    incomplete vocational vehicles and at least 50% of its tractors and
    incomplete tractors of the 2015 model year if the number of
    heavy-duty vehicles it sold in Canada is greater than 500;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at least 75% of its vocational vehicles and
    incomplete vocational vehicles and at least 75% of its tractors and
    incomplete tractors of the 2016 model year if the number of
    heavy-duty vehicles it sold in Canada is greater than 500; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all its 2017 and subsequent model year heavy-duty
    vehicles.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Credits --- heavy-duty vehicles of 2015
  and 2016 model years

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Unless a company elects to group all its vocational
  vehicles, incomplete vocational vehicles, tractors and incomplete
  tractors into fleets, credits obtained under paragraph (4)(a) or (b),
  as the case may be, for an averaging set of heavy-duty vehicles of the
  2015 or 2016 model year may only be used to offset a deficit incurred
  for that averaging set of the same model year, after which the credits
  are no longer valid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If all vehicles grouped into fleets

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (4),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} credits obtained for the 2014 model year may be
    used to offset a deficit for an averaging set of the 2015 model year
    if the company groups into fleets all its vehicles of the 2014 and
    2015 model years;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} credits obtained for the 2014 and 2015 model years
    may be used to offset a deficit for an averaging set of the 2016
    model year if the company groups into fleets all its vehicles of the
    2014, 2015 and 2016 model years; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} credits obtained for the 2014, 2015 and 2016 model
    years may be used to offset a deficit for an averaging set of the
    2017 or subsequent model year if the company groups into fleets all
    its vehicles of the 2014, 2015 and 2016 model years.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Early action credits

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (4), for an averaging
  set of the 2014, 2015 or 2016 model year, a company may use early
  action credits obtained in accordance with section 47 if the company
  groups into fleets all its vocational vehicles, incomplete vocational
  vehicles, tractors and incomplete tractors of the averaging set for
  the model year in which the early action credits are used.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceeding N~2~O or CH~4~ emission
  standard --- engines

  [(7.1)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 29(4) to (7) apply in respect of a
  company's heavy-duty engines that are covered by an EPA certificate if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the engines conform to a N~2~O or CH~4~ family
    emission limit that exceeds the N~2~O or CH~4~ emission standard
    applicable to engines of their model year under these Regulations;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of engines referred to in paragraph (a)
    that are sold in Canada by the company

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is greater than 100 but less than or equal to
      1000 and is more than twice the number of engines sold in the
      United States that are of the same engine family, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is greater than 1000 and exceeds the number of
      engines sold in the United States that are of the same engine
      family.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fleets --- engines

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} A company that manufactures or imports an engine that
  is covered by an EPA certificate must group all its engines into
  fleets in accordance with section 18 and must participate in the CO~2~
  emission credit system set out in sections 34 to 47 if the following
  conditions are met:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the engine conforms to a CO~2~ family certification
    level that exceeds the CO~2~ emission standard applicable to that
    engine's model year under these Regulations; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of engines referred to in paragraph (a)
    sold in Canada by the company

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is greater than 100 but less than or equal to
      1000 and is more than twice the number of engines sold in the
      United States that are of the same engine family, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is greater than 1000 and exceeds the number of
      engines sold in the United States that are of the same engine
      family.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Comply with CO~2~ emission credit
  system

  [(8.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if a company participates
  in the CO~2~ emission credit system set out in sections 47.1 to 47.5
  for its full-aero box van trailers that are covered by an EPA
  certificate, it must comply with the CO~2~ emission credit system
  provisions that relate to the emission standards referred to in
  subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fleets --- trailers

  [(8.2)]{.lawlabel} A company that manufactures or imports a full-aero
  box van trailer of the 2027 model year or a subsequent model year that
  is covered by an EPA certificate and conforms to a CO~2~ family
  emission limit that exceeds the CO~2~ emission standard applicable to
  the model year of that trailer under these Regulations must group all
  of its full-aero box van trailers of that model year into fleets or
  subfleets, as the case may be, in accordance with section 18 and
  participate in the CO~2~ emission credit system set out in sections
  47.1 to 47.5.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsection 153(3) of Act

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 153(3) of the Act, the
  provisions of the CFR that are applicable under an EPA certificate to
  a vehicle, engine or trailer referred to in subsection (1) correspond
  to the standards referred to in paragraphs (1)(a) to (e).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}EPA

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 153(3) of the Act,
  the EPA is the prescribed agency.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-186, s. 64
- SOR/2018-98, ss. 15, 60
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-13-20150716.html "Link to previous version of section 13"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Emission Control Systems]{.HTitleText3} {#h-801005 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}On-Road Vehicle and Engine Emission
Regulations

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  emission control system that is installed in a heavy-duty vehicle or
  heavy-duty engine for the purpose of conforming to the standards set
  out in these Regulations must comply with subsection 11(1) of the
  On-Road Vehicle and Engine Emission Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Defeat device

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A heavy-duty vehicle or heavy-duty engine must not be
  equipped with a defeat device.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- emergency vehicles

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), an emergency vehicle may be
  equipped with a defeat device if the device is one that is activated
  during emergency response operations to maintain speed, torque or
  power in either of the following circumstances:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the emission control system is in an abnormal
    state;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the device acts to maintain the emission control
    system in a normal state.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Test procedures

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 11(3) and (4) of the On-Road Vehicle and
  Engine Emission Regulations apply except that the test procedures in
  question are the ones set out in these Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 16
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-14-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 14"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Adjustable Parameters]{.HTitleText3} {#h-801019 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[adjustable
parameter]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[adjustable parameter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
  device, system or element of design that is capable of being adjusted
  to affect the emissions or performance of a heavy-duty vehicle,
  heavy-duty engine or trailer during emission testing or normal in-use
  operation, but does not include a device, system or element of design
  that is permanently sealed by the manufacturer or that is inaccessible
  using ordinary tools.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Standards

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A heavy-duty vehicle, heavy-duty engine or trailer
  that is equipped with adjustable parameters must conform to the
  applicable standards under these Regulations for any specification
  within the adjustable range.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fairings

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A tractor's roof fairing and a trailer's rear fairing
  are not adjustable parameters for the purposes of this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 17
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-15-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 15"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Air-conditioning Systems]{.HTitleText3} {#h-801029 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Standards

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** A heavy-duty vehicle or
heavy-duty incomplete vehicle that is equipped with an air-conditioning
system must conform to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} section 1819(h) of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter C,
  part 86, subpart S, of the CFR in the case of a Class 2B or Class 3
  heavy-duty vehicle or cab-complete vehicle, other than a vocational
  vehicle or incomplete vocational vehicle; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} section 115(e) of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U,
  part 1037, subpart B, of the CFR in the case of a vocational vehicle
  or incomplete vocational vehicle of the 2021 model year or a
  subsequent model year or a tractor or incomplete tractor.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 17
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 16"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Non-box Trailers and Non-aero Box Van Trailers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-801036 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Standards

- **[[16.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the 2020 model year and
  subsequent model years, every non-box trailer and non-aero box van
  trailer must be equipped with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an automatic tire inflation system or tire pressure
    monitoring system for each wheel that is mounted on an axle; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} tires with a tire rolling resistance level of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of non-box trailers,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} for the 2020 model year, 6 kg/t or less, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} for the 2021 model year and subsequent model
        years, 5.1 kg/t or less , and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of non-aero box van trailers,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} for the 2020 model year, 5.1 kg/t or less, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} for the 2021 model year and subsequent model
        years, 4.7 kg/t or less.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of tire rolling
  resistance level

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The tire rolling resistance level must be determined
  in accordance with the procedures described in sections 515(b) and
  520(c) of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1037, subpart F, of
  the CFR.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 17
:::

#### [Small Volume Companies]{.HTitleText3} {#h-801052 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2018-98, s. 18

\]
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company may elect, for a given model year, not to comply with the
  standards set out in any of subsections 26(1), (1.1), (1.2), (1.3)
  and (5) and 27(1), (1.1), (1.2) and (7), as the case may be, for its
  vocational vehicles, incomplete vocational vehicles, tractors and
  incomplete tractors and, in the case of vocational vehicles,
  incomplete vocational vehicles, tractors and incomplete tractors that
  are covered by an EPA certificate, the company may elect not to comply
  with subsection 13(4), if the following conditions are met:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it manufactured or imported in 2011 for sale in
    Canada in total less than 200 tractors and vocational vehicles;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of vocational vehicles and tractors of
    the model year in question that it manufactures or imports for sale
    in Canada is fewer than 200; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it reports this election in its end of model year
    report in accordance with section 48.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- engines

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A company that makes an election referred to in
  subsection (1) may also make one or both of the elections set out in
  one of the following paragraphs in respect of the engines of a given
  model year that are installed in its vocational vehicles and tractors
  referred to in subsection (1) if it reports the election in its end of
  model year report in accordance with section 48:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of heavy-duty engines other than those
    that are covered by an EPA certificate, not to comply with

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the N~2~O and CH~4~ emission standards set out in
      section 29, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ emission standards set out in section
      30 or subsection 31(1), (2) or (5); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of heavy-duty engines that are covered
    by an EPA certificate, not to comply with subsection 13(7.1) or (8).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}CO~2~ emission credit system

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company that makes an election referred to in
  subsection (1) or (1.1) must not participate in the CO~2~ emission
  credit system set out in sections 34 to 47 for the model year in
  question.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Merger

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a company merges with one or more companies after
  the day on which these Regulations come into force, the company that
  results from the merger may make an election referred to in
  subsection (1) or (1.1) if the combined number of vocational vehicles
  and tractors manufactured or imported for sale in Canada by the merged
  companies under each of paragraphs (1)(a) and (b) is fewer than 200.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a company acquires one or more companies, it must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the company made an election
    referred to in subsection (1) or (1.1) before the acquisition,
    recalculate the number of vocational vehicles and tractors that it
    manufactured or imported for sale in Canada under each of paragraphs
    (1)(a) and (b) by adding to that number the number of vocational
    vehicles and tractors that were manufactured or imported for sale in
    Canada by each of the acquired companies and report the total number
    in its first end of model year report following the acquisition; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the company makes an election
    referred to in subsection (1) or (1.1) after the acquisition,
    calculate the number of vocational vehicles and tractors that it
    manufactured or imported for sale in Canada under each of paragraphs
    (1)(a) and (b) by adding to that number the number of vocational
    vehicles and tractors that were manufactured or imported for sale in
    Canada by each of the acquired companies.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 19
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-17-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 17"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- trailers manufactured
before January 1, 2021

**[[17.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17.1 .sectionLabel}** A company may elect
not to comply with the standards set out in section 16.1 or 33.1, as the
case may be, for its trailers whose manufacture is completed before
January 1, 2021, if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in 2020 it manufactures or imports for sale in Canada
  fewer than 100 trailers in total; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it reports the election in its end of model year
  report in accordance with section 48.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 20
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- trailers of the 2020 to
2026 model years

**[[17.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17.2 .sectionLabel}** For trailers of the
2020 to 2026 model years, a company may elect not to comply with the
standards set out in section 16.1 or 33.1, as the case may be, for a
certain number of its trailers of a given model year if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of trailers in respect of which it makes
  the election does not exceed

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of box van trailers, 20% of the number
    of box van trailers of the model year in question that it
    manufactures or imports for sale in Canada or 25, whichever is less,
    and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of non-box trailers, 20% of the number
    of non-box trailers of the model year in question that it
    manufactures or imports for sale in Canada or 20, whichever is less;
    and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it reports the election in its end of model year
  report in accordance with section 48.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 20
:::

#### [Composition of Fleets]{.HTitleText3} {#h-801091 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[fleet]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  these Regulations, [[fleet]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} refers to the
  heavy-duty vehicles, heavy-duty engines and full-aero box van trailers
  that a company imports or manufactures in Canada for the purpose of
  sale in Canada to the first retail purchaser and that are grouped in
  accordance with this section for the purpose of conforming to sections
  21 to 23 or for the purpose of participation in the CO~2~ emission
  credit system set out in sections 34 to 47 or 47.1 to 47.5, as the
  case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company may elect to exclude from its fleets

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the heavy-duty vehicles, heavy-duty engines and
    full-aero box van trailers that it manufactures and that are to be
    used in Canada solely for the purpose of exhibition, demonstration,
    evaluation or testing, if it reports that election in its end of
    model year report; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the heavy-duty vehicles, heavy-duty engines and
    full-aero box van trailers that it imports and that are to be used
    in Canada solely for the purpose of exhibition, demonstration,
    evaluation or testing, if it makes a declaration in accordance with
    section 60 and it reports that election in its end of model year
    report.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fleet composition

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company may group all of its heavy-duty vehicles or
  heavy-duty engines of the same type and model year into more than one
  fleet as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of heavy-duty vehicles and subject to
    subsections (4) to (7), section 25 and subsections 26(6), (7.1) and
    (7.2) and 27(8.1) and (8.2), each fleet is composed solely of the
    vehicles referred to in one of the following subparagraphs or
    clauses, as the case may be:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty vehicles and
      cab-complete vehicles, excluding those that are vocational
      vehicles or incomplete vocational vehicles,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for vocational vehicles and incomplete
      vocational vehicles of the 2020 model year or an earlier model
      year,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} those vehicles that are or are to become light
        heavy-duty vehicles,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} those vehicles that are or are to become medium
        heavy-duty vehicles, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} those vehicles that are or are to become heavy
        heavy-duty vehicles,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} for multi-purpose vocational vehicles and
      incomplete vocational vehicles that are to become multi-purpose
      vocational vehicles of the 2021 model year or a subsequent model
      year that are equipped with a compression-ignition engine,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} those vehicles that are or are to become light
        heavy-duty vehicles,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} those vehicles that are or are to become medium
        heavy-duty vehicles, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} those vehicles that are or are to become heavy
        heavy-duty vehicles,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} for regional vocational vehicles and incomplete
      vocational vehicles that are to become regional vocational
      vehicles of the 2021 model year or a subsequent model year that
      are equipped with a compression-ignition engine,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} those vehicles that are or are to become light
        heavy-duty vehicles,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} those vehicles that are or are to become medium
        heavy-duty vehicles, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} those vehicles that are or are to become heavy
        heavy-duty vehicles,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} for urban vocational vehicles and incomplete
      vocational vehicles that are to become urban vocational vehicles
      of the 2021 model year or a subsequent model year that are
      equipped with a compression-ignition engine,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} those vehicles that are or are to become light
        heavy-duty vehicles,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} those vehicles that are or are to become medium
        heavy-duty vehicles, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} those vehicles that are or are to become heavy
        heavy-duty vehicles,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} for multi-purpose vocational vehicles and
      incomplete vocational vehicles that are to become multi-purpose
      vocational vehicles of the 2021 model year or a subsequent model
      year that are equipped with a spark-ignition engine,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} those vehicles that are or are to become light
        heavy-duty vehicles, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} those vehicles that are or are to become medium
        heavy-duty vehicles,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} for regional vocational vehicles and incomplete
      vocational vehicles that are to become regional vocational
      vehicles of the 2021 model year or a subsequent model year that
      are equipped with a spark-ignition engine,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} those vehicles that are or are to become light
        heavy-duty vehicles, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} those vehicles that are or are to become medium
        heavy-duty vehicles,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} for urban vocational vehicles and incomplete
      vocational vehicles that are to become urban vocational vehicles
      of the 2021 model year or a subsequent model year that are
      equipped with a spark-ignition engine,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} those vehicles that are or are to become light
        heavy-duty vehicles, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} those vehicles that are or are to become medium
        heavy-duty vehicles,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} Class 7 low-roof tractors and incomplete
      tractors,

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} Class 7 mid-roof tractors and incomplete
      tractors,

    - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} Class 7 high-roof tractors and incomplete
      tractors,

    - [(xii)]{.lawlabel} for Class 8 tractors and incomplete tractors of
      the 2020 model year or an earlier model year,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} those that are low-roof and are equipped with a
        day cab,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} those that are low-roof and are equipped with a
        sleeper cab,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} those that are mid-roof and are equipped with a
        day cab,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} those that are mid-roof and are equipped with a
        sleeper cab,

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} those that are high-roof and are equipped with
        a day cab, or

      - [(F)]{.lawlabel} those that are high-roof and are equipped with
        a sleeper cab,

    - [(xiii)]{.lawlabel} for Class 8 tractors and incomplete tractors
      of the 2021 model year or a subsequent model year that have a GCWR
      of less than 43 998 kg (97,000 pounds),

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} those that are low-roof and are equipped with a
        day cab,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} those that are low-roof and are equipped with a
        sleeper cab,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} those that are mid-roof and are equipped with a
        day cab,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} those that are mid-roof and are equipped with a
        sleeper cab,

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} those that are high-roof and are equipped with
        a day cab, or

      - [(F)]{.lawlabel} those that are high-roof and are equipped with
        a sleeper cab,

    - [(xiv)]{.lawlabel} for Class 8 tractors and incomplete tractors of
      the 2021 model year or a subsequent model year that have a GCWR of
      43 998 kg (97,000 pounds) or more, but less than 54 431 kg
      (120,000 pounds),

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} those that are low-roof and are equipped with a
        day cab,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} those that are low-roof and are equipped with a
        sleeper cab,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} those that are mid-roof and are equipped with a
        day cab,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} those that are mid-roof and are equipped with a
        sleeper cab,

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} those that are high-roof and are equipped with
        a day cab, or

      - [(F)]{.lawlabel} those that are high-roof and are equipped with
        a sleeper cab,

    - [(xv)]{.lawlabel} for Class 8 tractors and incomplete tractors of
      the 2021 model year or a subsequent model year that have a GCWR of
      54 431 kg (120,000 pounds) or more, other than those that are or
      are to become heavy-haul tractors,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} those that are low-roof and are equipped with a
        day cab,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} those that are low-roof and are equipped with a
        sleeper cab,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} those that are mid-roof and are equipped with a
        day cab,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} those that are mid-roof and are equipped with a
        sleeper cab,

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} those that are high-roof and are equipped with
        a day cab, or

      - [(F)]{.lawlabel} those that are high-roof and are equipped with
        a sleeper cab,

    - [(xvi)]{.lawlabel} heavy-haul tractors and incomplete tractors
      that are to become heavy-haul tractors;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of heavy-duty engines and subject to
    subsections (8) and (9), each fleet is composed solely of the
    engines referred to in one of the following subparagraphs:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} spark-ignition engines other than heavy
      heavy-duty engines of the 2021 model year or a subsequent model
      year,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} light heavy-duty engines that are
      compression-ignition engines and that are designed to be used in
      vocational vehicles and incomplete vocational vehicles,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} medium heavy-duty engines that are
      compression-ignition engines and that are designed to be used in
      vocational vehicles and incomplete vocational vehicles,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} heavy heavy-duty engines that are spark-ignition
      engines of the 2021 model year or a subsequent model year or
      compression-ignition engines and that are designed to be used in
      vocational vehicles and incomplete vocational vehicles,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} medium heavy-duty engines that are
      compression-ignition engines and that are designed to be used in
      tractors and incomplete tractors, or

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} heavy heavy-duty engines that are spark-ignition
      engines of the 2021 model year or a subsequent model year or
      compression-ignition engines and that are designed to be used in
      tractors and incomplete tractors;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of full-aero box van trailers and
    subject to subsection (10), each fleet is composed solely of the
    trailers referred to in one of the following subparagraphs:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} short dry box van trailers,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} long dry box van trailers,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} short refrigerated box van trailers,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} long refrigerated box van trailers.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty
  vehicles

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph (3)(a)(i), all of
  the following heavy-duty vehicles must be grouped into one separate
  fleet of Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty vehicles:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the 2020 model year or an earlier model year,
    hybrid vehicles that have regenerative braking;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} vehicles equipped with an engine that includes a
    Rankine-cycle or other bottoming cycle exhaust energy recovery
    system;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} electric vehicles;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} fuel cell vehicles;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} vehicles that are manufactured with innovative
    technologies; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} for the 2021 model year and subsequent model years,
    plug-in hybrid vehicles.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subfleets --- Class 2B and Class 3

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph (3)(a)(i) and
  subsection 20(3), the vehicles in the fleet that exceed the standards
  set out in subsection 20(1) and have more than one N~2~O or CH~4~
  family emission limit must be grouped into subfleets that include
  vehicles with identical N~2~O or CH~4~ family emission limits, as the
  case may be, and that are of the same test group.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vocational vehicles and tractors

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraphs (3)(a)(ii) to
  (xvi), all heavy-duty vehicles of a fleet must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, be hybrid vehicles of the 2020 model
    year or an earlier model year that have regenerative braking,
    vehicles equipped with an engine that includes a Rankine-cycle or
    other bottoming cycle exhaust energy recovery system, electric
    vehicles, fuel cell vehicles, vehicles manufactured with innovative
    technologies or plug-in hybrid vehicles of the 2021 model year or a
    subsequent model year; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be grouped into subfleets that include vehicles
    with identical CO~2~ family emission limits if the vehicles in the
    fleet have more than one family emission limit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vocational tractors

  [(6.1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (3)(a), if a company
  has made the election referred to in subsection 28(2) in respect of a
  number of its vocational tractors, those vocational tractors must be
  grouped into fleets composed solely of vocational tractors that meet
  the description set out in, as the case may be, subparagraph
  (3)(a)(ii), (iii), (iv), (v), (vi), (vii) or (viii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vocational vehicles --- election under
  subsection 26(1.2)

  [(6.2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraphs (3)(a)(ii) to
  (viii), vocational vehicles that are of a type referred to in the
  table to subsection 26(1.2) and in respect of which a company has made
  the election referred to in that subsection must be grouped into
  separate fleets with each fleet being composed solely of vehicles of
  the same type.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Roof heights, cab types and GVWR

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a vocational vehicle, incomplete vocational
  vehicle, tractor or incomplete tractor model straddles a roof height,
  cab type or GVWR division, a company may elect to group all those
  vehicles into the same fleet if they conform to the most stringent
  standards applicable to a vehicle in the fleet.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Heavy-duty engines

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (3)(b), all heavy-duty
  engines of a fleet must be of the same engine family or engine
  subfamily, as the case may be, and have, taking into account section
  205(e) of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1036, subpart C, of
  the CFR,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an identical CO~2~ family certification level; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} identical N~2~O and CH~4~ family emission limits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Engines not sold in United States

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (8), the CO~2~ family
  certification level and the N~2~O and CH~4~ family emission limits for
  the model year in question are determined using the engine sales in
  Canada if none of the engines in the engine family or engine
  subfamily, as the case may be, are sold in the United States.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subfleets --- full-aero box van
  trailers

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (3)(c), if the
  full-aero box van trailers in a fleet have more than one family
  emission limit, a company must group those trailers into subfleets
  that include trailers with an identical CO~2~ family emission limit.

#### [Grouping into Fleets]{.HTitleText3} {#h-801202 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Election applicable to all vehicles and
engines

**[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** If a company makes the
election referred to in subsection 22(4), 26(7), 27(8) or 33(1) for a
fleet of heavy-duty vehicles or heavy-duty engines that it manufactures
or imports, that election applies to all the vehicles and engines of
that fleet.

### [Class 2B and Class 3 Heavy-duty Vehicles]{.HTitleText2} {#h-801205 .Subheading}

#### [N~2~O and CH~4~ Emissions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-801206 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Standards

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the 2014 model year and subsequent model years, every Class 2B and
  Class 3 heavy-duty vehicle and cab-complete vehicle that is not a
  vocational vehicle or incomplete vocational vehicle must, for the
  duration of its useful life, have N~2~O and CH~4~ emission values that
  do not exceed 0.05 g/mile.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The N~2~O and CH~4~ emission values must be
  calculated in accordance with section 24.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fleet calculation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company that manufactures or imports vehicles
  referred to in subsection (1) whose N~2~O emission value or CH~4~
  emission value exceeds the emission standard set out in that
  subsection must group those vehicles of a given model year into a
  fleet and, if applicable, subfleets in accordance with section 18 and
  must calculate the N~2~O or CH~4~ emission deficit, as the case may
  be, for that fleet or each of those subfleets, expressed in megagrams
  of CO~2~, using the formula

  ::: {#801214 .Subsection}
  ((A -- B) × C × D × E) ÷ (1 000 000)

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is 0.05 g/mile;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the N~2~O or CH~4~ family emission limit for the fleet or
      subfleet, as the case may be, expressed in g/mile;

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of vehicles in the fleet or subfleet, as the case
      may be;

  [D]{.dfn}
  :   is the vehicle's useful life, expressed in miles; and

  [E]{.dfn}
  :   is the global warming potential and is equal to the following
      number of CO~2~ emission credits, expressed in megagrams of CO~2~,
      needed to offset a deficit of N~2~O or CH~4~:
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for each megagram of N~2~O, 298, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for each megagram of CH~4~,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the 2020 model year and earlier model
          years, 25, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for the 2021 model year and subsequent model
          years, 34.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Separate calculation

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (3), if both the
  N~2~O emission value and the CH~4~ emission value exceed 0.05 g/mile,
  the N~2~O and CH~4~ emission deficits must be calculated separately.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Family emission limit

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (3), every vehicle
  within a fleet or subfleet must conform to the N~2~O or CH~4~ family
  emission limit for the fleet or subfleet, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offsetting deficit

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The deficit calculated under subsection (3) must be
  offset by using the CO~2~ emission credits obtained in accordance with
  sections 34 to 47 for the averaging set in which the fleet is
  included.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No credits

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the company must not obtain
  CO~2~ emission credits with respect to N~2~O and CH~4~ emissions for
  the purpose of participation in the CO~2~ emission credit system set
  out in sections 34 to 47.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 22
:::

#### [CO~2~ Emissions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-801235 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Average standard

- **[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the 2014 model year and subsequent model years, a company must group
  all of its Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty vehicles and cab-complete
  vehicles --- excluding those that are vocational vehicles or
  incomplete vocational vehicles --- into a fleet based on model year in
  accordance with section 18 and must ensure that the fleet average
  CO~2~ emission value calculated in accordance with section 23 for that
  fleet does not exceed the applicable fleet average CO~2~ emission
  standard calculated in accordance with section 22 for the useful life
  of the vehicles of that fleet.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offsetting deficit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When a company incurs a deficit based on the
  calculation referred to in subsection (1), it must offset the deficit
  by using the CO~2~ emission credits obtained in accordance with
  sections 34 to 47 for the averaging set in which the fleet is
  included.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 23
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-21-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 21"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation of average standard

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (6), a company
  must calculate the fleet average CO~2~ emission standard for a given
  model year, expressed in grams of CO~2~ per mile and rounded to the
  nearest gram of CO~2~ per mile, for its fleet of Class 2B and Class 3
  heavy-duty vehicles and cab-complete vehicles --- excluding those that
  are vocational vehicles or incomplete vocational vehicles --- using
  the formula

  ::: {#801246 .Subsection}
  (Σ (A × B)) ÷ C

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the CO~2~ emission target value calculated for each vehicle
      subconfiguration in the fleet using the applicable formula set out
      in subsection (2) and rounded to the nearest gram of CO~2~ per
      mile;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of vehicles of that vehicle subconfiguration in the
      fleet; and

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of vehicles in the fleet.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vehicle subconfiguration

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), the CO~2~ emission target
  value for each vehicle subconfiguration in a fleet must be calculated
  using the applicable formula set out in one of the following
  paragraphs:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for vehicles that are equipped with a
    spark-ignition engine, the formula set out in column 2 of the table
    to this paragraph for the model year set out in column 1, where WF
    is the work factor for each vehicle subconfiguration, calculated
    using the formula set out in subsection (3) and rounded to the
    nearest pound;

    the formula set out in subsection (3) and rounded to the nearest
