              vocational vehicles that are to become medium heavy-duty
              vehicles, and

            - [(III)]{.lawlabel} 7.5 short tons for vocational vehicles
              that are heavy heavy-duty vehicles and incomplete
              vocational vehicles that are to become heavy heavy-duty
              vehicles,

        [E]{.dfn}
        :   is the number of vocational vehicles and incomplete
            vocational vehicles manufactured with the innovative
            technology in question in the fleet or subfleet, as the case
            may be, and

        [F]{.dfn}
        :   is the useful life of the vehicles of the fleet or subfleet,
            as the case may be, expressed in miles, or
        :::

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} for tractors and incomplete tractors:

        ::: {#801869 .Clause}
        (\[A -- (B × C)\] × D × E × F) ÷ (1 000 000)

        where

        [A]{.dfn}
        :   is the CO~2~ emission standard under subsection 27(1), (1.1)
            or (1.2) that applies to the tractors and incomplete
            tractors of the fleet or subfleet, as the case may be,
            expressed in grams of CO~2~ per short ton-mile,

        [B]{.dfn}
        :   is the CO~2~ family emission limit for the fleet or
            subfleet, as the case may be, expressed in grams of CO~2~
            per short ton-mile,

        [C]{.dfn}
        :   is the improvement factor calculated in accordance with
            sections 610(b)(1) and (c) of Title 40, chapter I,
            subchapter U, part 1037, subpart G, of the CFR, for the
            fleet or subfleet, as the case may be, of tractors and
            incomplete tractors or, in the case of tractors and
            incomplete tractors of the 2018 to 2020 model years, is the
            applicable improvement factor set out in section
            150(y)(2)(i) of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, part
            1037, subpart B, of the CFR, for the fleet or subfleet, as
            the case may be,

        [D]{.dfn}
        :   is the payload for the class of tractors, which is
            - [(I)]{.lawlabel} 12.5 short tons for Class 7 tractors or
              incomplete tractors,

            - [(II)]{.lawlabel} 19 short tons for Class 8 tractors and
              incomplete tractors of the 2020 model year or an earlier
              model year and for Class 8 tractors and incomplete
              tractors of the 2021 model year or a subsequent model year
              that have a GCWR of less than 54 431 kg (120,000 pounds),
              and

            - [(III)]{.lawlabel} 43 short tons for Class 8 tractors and
              incomplete tractors of the 2021 model year or a subsequent
              model year that have a GCWR of 54 431 kg (120,000 pounds)
              or more,

        [E]{.dfn}
        :   is the number of tractors and incomplete tractors
            manufactured with the innovative technology in question in
            the fleet or subfleet, as the case may be, and

        [F]{.dfn}
        :   is the useful life of the tractors or incomplete tractors of
            the fleet or subfleet, as the case may be, expressed in
            miles; or
        :::

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of heavy-duty engines,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} by using the following formula for engines tested
      on a chassis:

      ::: {#801882 .Subparagraph}
      ((A -- B) × C × D × E) ÷ (1 000 000)

      where

      [(A -- B)]{.dfn}
      :   is the difference between the in-use emission rate of the
          engine manufactured without the innovative technology and the
          in-use emission rate of the engine manufactured with the
          innovative technology, determined in accordance with chassis A
          to B testing or in-use A to B testing of pairs of vehicles
          equipped with the engines differing only with respect to the
          innovative technology in question, and expressed in grams of
          CO~2~ per short ton-mile,

      [C]{.dfn}
      :   is the number of vocational vehicles, incomplete vocational
          vehicles, tractors or incomplete tractors with engines
          manufactured with the innovative technology in the fleet,

      [D]{.dfn}
      :   is the payload, if applicable, for the class of vehicles,
          which is
          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} 2.85 short tons for vocational vehicles
            that are light heavy-duty vehicles and incomplete vocational
            vehicles that are to become light heavy-duty vehicles,

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} 5.6 short tons for vocational vehicles that
            are medium heavy-duty vehicles and incomplete vocational
            vehicles that are to become medium heavy-duty vehicles,

          - [(C)]{.lawlabel} 7.5 short tons for vocational vehicles that
            are heavy heavy-duty vehicles and incomplete vocational
            vehicles that are to become heavy heavy-duty vehicles,

          - [(D)]{.lawlabel} 12.5 short tons for Class 7 tractors and
            incomplete tractors,

          - [(E)]{.lawlabel} 19 short tons for Class 8 tractors and
            incomplete tractors of the 2020 model year or an earlier
            model year and for Class 8 tractors and incomplete tractors
            of the 2021 model year or a subsequent model year that have
            a GCWR of less than 54 431 kg (120,000 pounds), and

          - [(F)]{.lawlabel} 43 short tons for Class 8 tractors and
            incomplete tractors of the 2021 model year or a subsequent
            model year that have a GCWR of 54 431 kg (120,000 pounds) or
            more, and

      [E]{.dfn}
      :   is the useful life of the vehicles of the fleet, expressed in
          miles,
      :::

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} by using the following formula for engines
      tested on an engine dynamometer:

      ::: {#801895 .Subparagraph}
      ((A -- B) × C × D × E) ÷ (1 000 000)

      where

      [(A -- B)]{.dfn}
      :   is the difference between the in-use emission rate of the
          engine manufactured without the innovative technology and the
          in-use emission rate of the engine manufactured with the
          innovative technology, determined in accordance with engine
          dynamometer A to B testing of pairs of engines differing only
          with respect to the innovative technology in question and
          expressed in grams of CO~2~ per brake horsepower-hour,

      [C]{.dfn}
      :   is the transient cycle conversion factor calculated in
          accordance with the applicable variable "CF" in section 705(b)
          of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1036, subpart H, of
          the CFR, except that a reference to "production volumes" in
          that section must be read as a reference to "number of engines
          of that engine family that a company imports or manufactures
          in Canada for the purpose of sale in Canada to the first
          retail purchaser",

      [D]{.dfn}
      :   is the number of engines manufactured with the innovative
          technology in the fleet,

      [E]{.dfn}
      :   is the useful life of the engines of the fleet, expressed in
          miles, or
      :::

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} by substituting the result obtained in
      accordance with paragraph 35(1)(d) with the result determined in
      accordance with the following formula:

      ::: {#801902 .Subparagraph}
      (\[A -- (B × C)\] × D × E × F) ÷ (1 000 000)

      where

      [A]{.dfn}
      :   is the CO~2~ emission standard that applies to the fleet of
          heavy-duty engines under section 30 or subsection 31(2), as
          the case may be, expressed in grams of CO~2~ per brake
          horsepower-hour,

      [B]{.dfn}
      :   is the CO~2~ family certification level for the fleet, subject
          to subsection 35(3), expressed in grams of CO~2~ per brake
          horsepower-hour,

      [C]{.dfn}
      :   is the improvement factor calculated in accordance with
          section 610(b)(1) of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, part
          1036, subpart G, of the CFR, based on results of A to B
          testing, chassis A to B testing or in-use A to B testing of
          pairs of engines using an engine dynamometer or of pairs of
          vehicles equipped with the engines in question, as the case
          may be, differing only with respect to the innovative
          technology in question,

      [D]{.dfn}
      :   is the transient cycle conversion factor calculated in
          accordance with the applicable variable "CF" in section 705(b)
          of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1036, subpart H, of
          the CFR, except that a reference to "production volumes" in
          that section must be read as a reference to "number of engines
          of that engine family that a company imports or manufactures
          in Canada for the purpose of sale in Canada to the first
          retail purchaser",

      [E]{.dfn}
      :   is the number of engines manufactured with the innovative
          technology in the fleet, and

      [F]{.dfn}
      :   is the useful life of the engines of the fleet, expressed in
          miles.
      :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation --- alternative procedure

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the five-cycle credit value referred to in the
  description of A in the formula set out in paragraph (1)(a) cannot
  adequately measure the emission reduction attributable to an
  innovative technology, the company may calculate that five-cycle
  credit value using the alternative procedure set out in section
  1869(d) of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter C, part 86, of the CFR, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle that is covered by an EPA
    certificate, the alternative procedure has been approved by the EPA
    for that technology and the company provides the Minister with
    evidence of the EPA approval; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle that is not covered by an
    EPA certificate, the company provides the Minister with evidence
    demonstrating that the alternative procedure provides a more
    representative benefit for that technology.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, ss. 45, 60, 61
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-41-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 41"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Averaging Sets]{.HTitleText3} {#h-801916 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation

**[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** The credits or deficits
for each averaging set of heavy-duty vehicles or heavy-duty engines are
calculated by adding the credits obtained and deficits incurred for all
fleets and, if applicable, subfleets of that averaging set. The credits
and deficits must be added together before rounding and the result must
be rounded to the nearest megagram of CO~2~.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 46
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-42-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 42"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of credits or deficits

**[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** A company obtains credits
or incurs deficits for an averaging set of heavy-duty vehicles or
heavy-duty engines on the day on which the company submits the end of
model year report for that model year.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of credits --- time limitations

- **[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of section 45, credits obtained by a company for an
  averaging set of heavy-duty vehicles or heavy-duty engines of a given
  model year may be used only in respect of the same averaging set of
  heavy-duty vehicles or heavy-duty engines of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a model year that is up to three model years before
    the model year for which the credits are obtained; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a model year that is up to five model years after
    the model year for which the credits are obtained.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Election --- subsection 26(1.2)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(b) does not apply in the case of
  vocational vehicles in respect of which a company has made the
  election referred to in subsection 26(1.2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- heavy-duty vehicles

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(b), credits obtained by a
  company for an averaging set of vocational vehicles that are light
  heavy-duty vehicles or medium heavy-duty vehicles of the 2018 to 2021
  model years may be used only in respect of the same averaging set of
  heavy-duty vehicles of any subsequent model year, ending with the 2027
  model year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- heavy-duty engines

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(b), credits obtained by a
  company for an averaging set of medium heavy-duty engines or heavy
  heavy-duty engines of the 2018 to 2024 model years may be used only in
  respect of the same averaging set of heavy-duty engines of any
  subsequent model year, ending with the 2030 model year.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 47
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-44-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 44"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deficits

- **[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (4) and (6),
  a company must use the credits obtained for an averaging set of
  heavy-duty vehicles or heavy-duty engines of a given model year to
  offset any outstanding deficits incurred for that averaging set.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remaining credits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except in the case of an averaging set of vocational
  vehicles in respect of which a company has made the election referred
  to in subsection 26(1.2), a company may bank any remaining credits to
  offset a future deficit for that averaging set or it may transfer the
  remaining credits to another company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Credit multiplier

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Credits obtained by a company for any of the
  averaging sets of the 2016 to 2020 model years referred to in
  paragraph (a) or (b) that are used to offset a deficit incurred for
  the same averaging set of the 2021 model year or a subsequent model
  year may be multiplied by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 1.36, in the case of the averaging sets referred to
    in paragraph (b), (e) or (f) of the definition [[averaging
    set]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 1(1); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 1.25, in the case of the averaging set referred to
    in paragraph (a) of the definition [[averaging
    set]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 1(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offsetting

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), a company may offset a
  deficit that it incurs for an averaging set of heavy-duty vehicles or
  heavy-duty engines with an equivalent number of credits obtained in
  accordance with section 35 or transferred from another company for
  that averaging set.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transfer of credits

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A company that obtains credits in accordance with
  sections 37 to 40 for an averaging set may transfer them to one of its
  other averaging sets to offset a deficit incurred in accordance with
  any of paragraphs 35(1)(a) to (d) if the following conditions are met:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the company obtained credits in accordance with
    section 37, they are used to offset any deficits for other vehicles
    in that averaging set before transferring any remaining credits to
    other averaging sets; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} not more than 6 000 Mg of CO~2~ emission credits
    per model year are transferred between any of the following groups
    of averaging sets:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} averaging sets of spark-ignition engines, light
      heavy-duty engines that are compression-ignition engines and light
      heavy-duty vehicles,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} averaging sets of medium heavy-duty engines that
      are compression-ignition engines and medium heavy-duty vehicles,
      or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} averaging sets of heavy heavy-duty engines that
      are compression-ignition engines and heavy heavy-duty vehicles.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The credit transfer limit between the groups set out
  in paragraph (4)(b) does not apply when the credits are used between
  the averaging sets of the engines and vehicles referred to in each of
  the subparagraphs of that paragraph.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offsetting deficits --- time limit

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A company must offset a deficit incurred for an
  averaging set of heavy-duty vehicles or heavy-duty engines of a given
  model year no later than the day on which the company submits the end
  of model year report in accordance with section 48 for vehicles or
  engines of the third model year after the model year for which the
  company incurred the deficit.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 48
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-45-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 45"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition or merger

- **[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company that acquires another company or that results from a merger of
  companies must offset any outstanding deficit of the purchased or
  merged companies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ceasing activities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a company ceases to manufacture, import or sell
  heavy-duty vehicles or heavy-duty engines, it must offset all
  outstanding deficits for its averaging sets before submitting its last
  end of model year report.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 49(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-46-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 46"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Early Action Credits]{.HTitleText3} {#h-801967 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligibility

- **[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company may obtain early action credits for an averaging set of
  heavy-duty vehicles or heavy-duty engines that are
  compression-ignition engines of the 2013 model year or for an
  averaging set of heavy-duty engines that are spark-ignition engines of
  the 2015 model year, if the number of credits calculated for that
  averaging set is greater than the number of deficits incurred for that
  model year and the company reports the credits

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in its 2014 end of model year report, in the case
    of heavy-duty vehicles and heavy-duty engines that are
    compression-ignition engines; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in its 2016 end of model year report, in the case
    of heavy-duty engines that are spark-ignition engines.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electric vehicles

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company may obtain early action credits by grouping
  its fleets of electric vehicles of the 2011 to 2013 model years into
  the applicable averaging sets and if the company reports the credits
  in its 2014 end of model year report.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}What to include

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of obtaining early action credits, a
  company must group

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} into the applicable fleet, all its vocational
    vehicles, tractors, Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty vehicles
    equipped with a spark-ignition engine or Class 2B and Class 3
    heavy-duty vehicles equipped with a compression-ignition engine,
    except in the case of electric vehicles; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} into the applicable averaging set, all its
    heavy-duty engines.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A company obtains early action credits on the day on
  which its 2014 end of model year report is submitted in the case of
  heavy-duty vehicles and heavy-duty engines that are
  compression-ignition engines, and on the day on which its 2016 end of
  model year report is submitted in the case of heavy-duty engines that
  are spark-ignition engines.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Early action credits obtained or deficits incurred
  within each averaging set for the following fleets must be calculated
  in accordance with sections 35 to 41, as applicable, using the
  following standards:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of heavy-duty vehicles and heavy-duty
    engines that are compression-ignition engines of the 2013 model
    year, the emission standards applicable to the 2014 model year;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of electric vehicles of the 2011 to
    2013 model years, the emission standards applicable to the 2014
    model year; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of heavy-duty engines that are
    spark-ignition engines of the 2015 model year, the emission
    standards applicable to the 2016 model year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Credit multiplier

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Early action credits obtained for vocational
  vehicles, tractors or heavy-duty engines may be multiplied by 1.5 if
  the company does not use the additional credit multiplier referred to
  in subsection 38(4), 39(3) or 40(2) for the same vehicles or engines.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time limit

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Early action credits may be used as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} credits obtained for heavy-duty vehicles or
    heavy-duty engines that are compression-ignition engines of the 2013
    model year may be used for the 2014 to 2018 model years;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} credits obtained for electric vehicles of the 2011
    to 2013 model years may be used for the 2014 to 2018 model years;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} credits obtained for heavy-duty engines that are
    spark-ignition engines of the 2015 model year may be used for the
    2016 to 2020 model years.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The rules set out in sections 45 and 46 with respect
  to credits also apply to early action credits.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 50
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-47-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 47"){.PITLink}
:::

### [CO~2~ Emission Credit System --- Full-aero Box Van Trailers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-801997 .Subheading}

#### [Calculation of Credits and Deficits]{.HTitleText3} {#h-801998 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Credits

- **[[47.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph
  162(1)(b)(i) of the Act, a company obtains CO~2~ emission credits if
  the CO~2~ emissions for a fleet or subfleet, as the case may be, of
  full-aero box van trailers of a given model year are lower than the
  CO~2~ emission standard applicable to the trailers of that fleet or
  subfleet, as the case may be, for that model year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deficits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company incurs deficits if the CO~2~ emissions for
  a fleet or subfleet, as the case may be, of full-aero box van trailers
  of a given model year are higher than the CO~2~ emission standard
  applicable to the trailers of that fleet or subfleet, as the case may
  be, for that model year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}End of model year report

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The company must report any credits obtained and any
  deficits incurred in its end of model year report in accordance with
  section 48.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 51
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation

**[[47.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47.2 .sectionLabel}** A company must
calculate the credits or deficits for each of its fleets or subfleets,
as the case may be, of full-aero box van trailers using the formula

::: {#802010 .Section}
ECD = ((A -- B) × C × D × E) ÷ (1 000 000)

where

[ECD]{.dfn}
:   is the number of credits, if the result is positive, or the number
    of deficits, if the result is negative, expressed in megagrams of
    CO~2~;

[A]{.dfn}
:   is the CO~2~ emission standard under subsection 33.1(1) that applies
    to the trailers of the fleet or subfleet, as the case may be,
    expressed in grams of CO~2~ per short ton-mile;

[B]{.dfn}
:   is the CO~2~ family emission limit for the fleet or subfleet, as the
    case may be, expressed in grams of CO~2~ per short ton-mile;

[C]{.dfn}
:   is the payload for the type of trailer, which is
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 10 short tons for short box van trailers, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 19 short tons for long box van trailers;

[D]{.dfn}
:   is the number of trailers in the fleet or subfleet, as the case may
    be; and

[E]{.dfn}
:   is 250,000 miles.
:::

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 51
:::

#### [Averaging Sets]{.HTitleText3} {#h-802022 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation

- **[[47.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The credits or deficits for each
  averaging set of full-aero box van trailers are calculated by adding
  the credits obtained and deficits incurred for all fleets and, if
  applicable, subfleets of that averaging set. The credits and deficits
  must be added together before rounding and the result must be rounded
  to the nearest megagram of CO~2~.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of credits or deficits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company obtains credits or incurs deficits for an
  averaging set of full-aero box van trailers on the day on which the
  company submits the end of model year report for that model year.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 51
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offsetting

- **[[47.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47.4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company must use the credits obtained for an averaging set of
  full-aero box van trailers of a given model year to offset any
  outstanding deficits incurred for that averaging set. Deficits are
  offset by an equivalent number of credits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of credits --- time limitations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Credits obtained by a company for an averaging set of
  full-aero box van trailers of a given model year may be used only to
  offset an outstanding deficit in respect of the same averaging set of
  full-aero box van trailers of a model year that is up to three model
  years before the model year for which the credits are obtained. The
  company must offset the deficit not later than the day on which the
  company submits the end of model year report in accordance with
  section 48.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 51
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition or merger

- **[[47.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47.5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company that acquires another company or that results from a merger of
  companies must offset any outstanding deficit of the purchased or
  merged companies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ceasing activities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a company ceases to manufacture, import or sell
  full-aero box van trailers, it must offset all outstanding deficits
  for its averaging sets before submitting its last end of model year
  report.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 51
:::

## [Reports]{.HTitleText1} {#h-802044 .Part}

### [End of Model Year Report]{.HTitleText2} {#h-802045 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deadline

- **[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company must submit to the Minister an end of model year report,
  signed by a person who is authorized to act on behalf of the company,
  for all heavy-duty vehicles and heavy-duty engines of the 2014 model
  year or of a subsequent model year, and all trailers of the 2020 model
  year or a subsequent model year, that it imported or manufactured in
  Canada, not later than June 30 of the calendar year following the
  calendar year that corresponds to the model year in question.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusion

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a company in
  respect of any of their heavy-duty vehicles or heavy-duty incomplete
  vehicles that they have altered in accordance with subsection 11(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The end of model year report must indicate the model
  year for which the report is made and must include the following
  statements by the company for its vehicles, engines and trailers, as
  applicable:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty
    vehicles and cab-complete vehicles, excluding those that are
    vocational vehicles or incomplete vocational vehicles,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the fleet average CO~2~ emission
      standard, that all its vehicles are grouped into one or more
      fleets in accordance with section 18, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the N~2~O and CH~4~ emission
      standards, that its vehicles

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} conform to the applicable N~2~O and CH~4~
        emission standards,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} are covered by an EPA certificate, bear the
        label referred to in subparagraph 53(d)(i) and conform either to
        the emission standards referred to in the EPA certificate or to
        a N~2~O or CH~4~ family emission limit, as the case may be, that
        is lower than the N~2~O or CH~4~ emission standard applicable to
        the model year of the vehicles under these Regulations, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} are grouped into one or more fleets in
        accordance with section 18 for the purpose of offsetting a
        deficit in accordance with subsection 20(5);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of tractors and vocational vehicles,
    that its vehicles

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} conform to the applicable CO~2~ emission
      standard,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} are covered by an EPA certificate, bear the
      label referred to in subparagraph 53(d)(i) and conform either to
      the emission standard referred to in the EPA certificate or to a
      CO~2~ family emission limit that is lower than the CO~2~ emission
      standard applicable to the model year of the vehicles under these
      Regulations,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} are covered by an EPA certificate, bear the
      label referred to in subparagraph 53(d)(i), conform to a CO~2~
      family emission limit that exceeds the CO~2~ emission standard
      applicable to the model year of the vehicles under these
      Regulations and are not grouped into one or more fleets in
      accordance with subsection 13(4),

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} are grouped into one or more fleets in
      accordance with section 18 for the purpose of participation in the
      CO~2~ emission credit system,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} are exempted under subsection 17(1),

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} in the case of vocational tractors, conform to
      the emission standards applicable to vocational vehicles in
      accordance with section 28,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} are equipped with an engine that meets the
      conditions set out in subsection 12.2(2) and conforms to the
      standards referred to in subsection 12.2(3), or

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} are equipped with an engine that conforms to
      the alternate standards referred to in subsection 12.2(4);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of heavy-duty engines,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the CO~2~ emission standard, that
      its engines

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} conform to the applicable CO~2~ emission
        standard,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} are covered by an EPA certificate, bear the
        label referred to in subparagraph 53(d)(ii) and conform either
        to the emission standard referred to in the EPA certificate or
        to a CO~2~ family certification level that is lower than the
        CO~2~ emission standard applicable to the model year of the
        engines under these Regulations,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} are covered by an EPA certificate, bear the
        label referred to in subparagraph 53(d)(ii), conform to a CO~2~
        family certification level that exceeds the CO~2~ emission
        standard applicable to the model year of the engines under these
        Regulations and are not grouped into one or more fleets in
        accordance with subsection 13(8),

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} are grouped into one or more fleets in
        accordance with section 18 for the purpose of participation in
        the CO~2~ emission credit system, or

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} are exempted under subparagraph 17(1.1)(a)(ii)
        or paragraph 17(1.1)(b), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the N~2~O and CH~4~ emission
      standards, that its engines

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} conform to the applicable N~2~O and CH~4~
        emission standards,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} are covered by an EPA certificate, bear the
        label referred to in subparagraph 53(d)(ii) and conform either
        to the emission standards referred to in the EPA certificate or
        to a N~2~O or CH~4~ family emission limit, as the case may be,
        that is lower than the N~2~O or CH~4~ emission standard
        applicable to the model year of the engines under these
        Regulations,

      - [(B.1)]{.lawlabel} are covered by an EPA certificate, bear the
        U.S. engine information label referred to in subparagraph
        53(d)(ii), conform to a N~2~O or CH~4~ family emission limit, as
        the case may be, that exceeds the N~2~O or CH~4~ emission
        standard applicable to the model year of the engines under these
        Regulations and, in accordance with subsection 13(7.1), do not
        conform to subsections 29(4) to (7),

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} are grouped into one or more fleets in
        accordance with section 18 for the purpose of offsetting a
        deficit in accordance with subsection 29(6), or

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} are exempted under subparagraph 17(1.1)(a)(i)
        or paragraph 17(1.1)(b); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of trailers, that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} its trailers conform to the applicable standards
      set out in subsection 16.1(1) or section 33.1, as the case may be,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} its trailers are covered by an EPA certificate,
      bear the U.S. emission control information label referred to in
      subparagraph 53(d)(i) and conform either to the emission standard
      referred to in the EPA certificate or, in the case of full-aero
      box van trailers, to a CO~2~ family emission limit that is lower
      than the CO~2~ emission standard applicable to the model year of
      the trailers under these Regulations,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} its full-aero box van trailers are grouped into
      one or more fleets in accordance with section 18 for the purpose
      of participation in the CO~2~ emission credit system,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} its trailers are exempted under section 17.1, or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a certain number of its trailers are exempted
      under section 17.2.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement when conforming to standards

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an end of model year report contains any of the
  statements referred to in clause (2)(a)(ii)(A), subparagraph
  (2)(b)(i), clauses (2)(c)(i)(A) and (ii)(A) and subparagraph (2)(d)(i)
  for a given model year, it must contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of heavy-duty vehicles or heavy-duty
    engines for each type referred to in paragraph 18(3)(a) or (b) or
    the number of trailers for each type referred to in subsection
    16.1(1) or 33.1(1) or (2), as the case may be; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ emission standard and, if applicable, the
    N~2~O and CH~4~ emission standards to which the vehicles, engines or
    trailers, as the case may be, conform.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement when covered by EPA
  certificate

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If an end of model year report contains any of the
  statements referred to in clause (2)(a)(ii)(B), subparagraphs
  (2)(b)(ii) and (iii), clauses (2)(c)(i)(B) and (C) and (ii)(B) and
  (B.1) and subparagraph (2)(d)(ii) for a given model year, it must
  contain the following information for each type of heavy-duty vehicle,
  heavy-duty engine or trailer, as the case may be:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of vehicles or engines for each type
    referred to in paragraph 18(3)(a) or (b) or the number of trailers
    for each type referred to in subsection 16.1(1) or 33.1(1) or (2);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of vehicles and trailers, the CO~2~
    family emission limit and, in the case of engines, the CO~2~ family
    certification level;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the number of vehicles, trailers or engines for
    each CO~2~ family emission limit or CO~2~ family certification
    level, as the case may be;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the N~2~O or CH~4~ family emission
    limit;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the number of vehicles or engines
    for each N~2~O or CH~4~ family emission limit; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if an end of model year report contains the
    statement referred to in clause (2)(c)(i)(C) or (ii)(B.1), the
    number of heavy-duty engines sold in the United States that are of
    the same engine family.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement for exempted tractors and
  vocational vehicles

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If an end of model year report contains the statement
  referred to in subparagraph (2)(b)(v) for a given model year, the
  report must contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of tractors and vocational vehicles that
    the company manufactured or imported in 2011 for sale in Canada; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2018-98, s. 52\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the number of tractors and vocational vehicles that
    the company manufactured or imported for sale in Canada for that
    model year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement --- vehicle referred to in
  subsection 12.2(2)

  [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} If an end of model year report contains the
  statement referred to in subparagraph (2)(b)(vii) for a given model
  year, the report must contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of vocational vehicles and tractors that
    the company manufactured or imported for sale in Canada for that
    model year; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the model year of the engine installed in each
    vehicle and, in each case, which of the conditions set out in
    paragraphs 12.2(2)(a) to (d) the engine meets.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement --- vehicle referred to in
  subsection 12.2(4)

  [(5.2)]{.lawlabel} If an end of model year report contains the
  statement referred to in subparagraph (2)(b)(viii) for a given model
  year, the report must contain the number of hybrid vehicles that the
  company elected to equip with an engine that conforms to the alternate
  standards referred to in subsection 12.2(4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement --- exempted engines

  [(5.3)]{.lawlabel} If an end of model year report contains the
  statement referred to in clause (2)(c)(i)(E) or (ii)(D) for a given
  model year, the report must contain the number of engines that are
  installed in the vocational vehicles and tractors referred to in
  subsection 17(1) that the company has elected to exempt under
  subsection 17(1.1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement --- exempted trailers

  [(5.4)]{.lawlabel} If an end of model year report contains the
  statement referred to in subparagraph (2)(d)(iv) or (v) for a given
  model year, the report must contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the statement referred to in
    subparagraph (2)(d)(iv), the number of trailers that the company
    manufactured or imported for sale in Canada in 2020; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the statement referred to in
    subparagraph (2)(d)(v),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for each type of trailer, the number of trailers
      that the company manufactured or imported for sale in Canada for
      the model year in question, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the number of trailers that the company has
      elected to exempt under section 17.2 for that model year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternative standards for engines ---
  section 25

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the company includes in a fleet, in accordance
  with section 25, spark-ignition engines that are not installed in
  vehicles or that are installed in heavy-duty incomplete vehicles that
  are not cab-complete vehicles, it must provide the number of those
  engines, along with the total number of engines in that vehicle fleet
  --- whether they are installed in vehicles or not --- that are of the
  same model year, design and hardware.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If an end of model year report contains any statement
  referred to in subparagraph (2)(a)(i), clause (2)(a)(ii)(C),
  subparagraph (2)(b)(iv), clauses (2)(c)(i)(D) and (ii)(C) and
  subparagraph (2)(d)(iii) for a given model year, the report must
  contain the following information for each averaging set:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, a statement that the company has
    elected to exclude from its fleets heavy-duty vehicles, heavy-duty
    engines or full-aero box van trailers in accordance with subsection
    18(2);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, a statement that the company has
    elected to comply with the alternative standards for vocational
    vehicles equipped with spark-ignition engines referred to in
    subsection 26(6);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an identification of all fleets and subfleets
    referred to in section 18 within the averaging set;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in relation to CO~2~ emission standards and, if
    applicable, the N~2~O and CH~4~ emission standards:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty vehicles and
      cab-complete vehicles, excluding those that are vocational
      vehicles or incomplete vocational vehicles,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the N~2~O and CH~4~ emission standards
        applicable to each fleet,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the fleet average CO~2~ emission standard,
        determined for A in the formula set out in paragraph 35(1)(a),

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ emission target value for each
        vehicle subconfiguration of each fleet, determined for A in the
        formula set out in subsection 22(1),

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} the work factor for each vehicle
        subconfiguration calculated in accordance with subsection 22(3),
        and

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the GVWR, curb weight, GCWR, type of
        transmission, gear ratio, axle ratio and type of engine for each
        vehicle configuration,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for vocational vehicles and incomplete
      vocational vehicles, the CO~2~ emission standard that applies to
      the vehicles of each fleet or subfleet, as the case may be,
      determined for A in paragraph 35(1)(b),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} for tractors and incomplete tractors, the CO~2~
      emission standard that applies to the vehicles of each fleet or
      subfleet, as the case may be, determined for A in paragraph
      35(1)(c),

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} for heavy-duty engines, the CO~2~ emission
      standard and N~2~O and CH~4~ emission standards that apply to the
      engines of each fleet, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} for full-aero box van trailers, the CO~2~
      emission standard that applies to the trailers of each fleet or
      subfleet, as the case may be, determined for A in section 47.2;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in relation to CO~2~ emissions, and if applicable,
    to N~2~O and CH~4~ emissions,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for each fleet of Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty
      vehicles and cab-complete vehicles --- excluding those that are
      vocational vehicles or incomplete vocational vehicles --- the
      following values:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the fleet average CO~2~ emission value,
        determined for B in the formula set out in paragraph 35(1)(a),

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ emission value for each vehicle
        configuration, determined for A in the formula set out in
        subsection 23(1) and taking into account subsection 23(2), and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the N~2~O or CH~4~ family
        emission limit, determined for B in the formula set out in
        subsection 20(3),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for each fleet of vocational vehicles and
      incomplete vocational vehicles, the CO~2~ family emission limit
      for each subfleet, determined for B in the formula set out in
      paragraph 35(1)(b),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} for each fleet of tractors and incomplete
      tractors, the CO~2~ family emission limit for each subfleet,
      determined for B in the formula set out in paragraph 35(1)(c),

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} for each fleet of heavy-duty engines,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ family certification level,
        determined for B in the formula set out in paragraph 35(1)(d),
        and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the N~2~O and CH~4~ family
        emission limits, determined for B in the formula set out in
        subsection 29(4), and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} for each fleet of full-aero box van trailers, the
      CO~2~ family emission limit for each fleet or subfleet, as the
      case may be;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the number of heavy-duty vehicles or heavy-duty
    engines in each averaging set, fleet, subfleet, vehicle
    configuration, engine configuration and the number of vehicles in
    each vehicle subconfiguration;

  - [(f.1)]{.lawlabel} the number of full-aero box van trailers in each
    averaging set, fleet and subfleet;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the evidence of the variable F
    referred to in the formula set out in paragraph 24(3)(b);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the evidence of the EPA approval
    referred to in paragraph 27(6)(a);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the evidence referred to in
    paragraph 27(6)(b);

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, evidence of the EPA approval
    referred to in paragraph (a) of the description of A in the formula
    set out in subsection 40(1);

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the evidence referred to in
    paragraph (b) of the description of A in the formula set out in
    subsection 40(1);

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the evidence of the EPA approval
    referred to in paragraph 41(2)(a);

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the evidence referred to in
    paragraph 41(2)(b);

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the number of CO~2~ emission credits
    calculated in accordance with subsection 29(8) for an N~2~O family
    emission limit that is less than 0.04 g/BHP-hr;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} in the case of heavy-duty vehicles or heavy-duty
    engines, the number of credits and deficits, calculated in
    accordance with section 35 for each fleet and subfleet, and the
    value of each variable --- along with its description --- used in
    calculating them;

  - [(o.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of full-aero box van trailers, the
    number of credits and deficits, calculated in accordance with
    section 47.2 for each fleet and subfleet, and the value of each
    variable --- along with its description --- used in calculating
    them;

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} the number of additional credits, calculated in
    accordance with section 38 for each fleet, including the following
    values:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the improvement factor,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the emission rate A,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the emission rate B,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the modelling result B, along with the value and
      description of each parameter used in determining that result, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the values determined for C, D and E;

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} the number of additional credits, calculated in
    accordance with section 39 for each fleet and subfleet, and the
    value of each variable used in calculating them;

  - [(r)]{.lawlabel} the number of additional credits, calculated in
    accordance with section 40, for each fleet and subfleet, and the
    value of each variable used in calculating them;

  - [(s)]{.lawlabel} the number of additional credits, calculated in
    accordance with section 41, for each fleet and subfleet, and the
    value of each variable used in calculating them;

  - [(t)]{.lawlabel} an identification of every instance in each fleet
    or subfleet, as the case may be, when the following credit
    multipliers were used:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the 1.5 credit multiplier referred to in section
      37 and subsections 38(4), 39(3) and 40(2), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the credit multipliers referred to in section
      40.1;

  - [(u)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the number of CO~2~ emission credits
    and early action credits that are used to offset a deficit incurred
    for the model year or an outstanding deficit and the averaging set
    and the model year for which the credits were obtained;

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, an accounting of the CO~2~ emission
    credits, early action credits and deficits and, for each averaging
    set in respect of which the credit multiplier referred to in
    paragraph 45(2.1)(a) or (b) was used, a statement that the
    multiplier was used; and

  - [(w)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of paragraphs 13(4)(a) and (b), in
    the company's end of model year reports for the 2015 and 2016 model
    years, the percentage of its vocational vehicles and incomplete
    vocational vehicles and the percentage of its tractors and
    incomplete tractors that are grouped into one or more fleets in
    accordance with section 18 for the purpose of participation in the
    CO~2~ emission credit system.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement --- subparagraph (2)(b)(vi)

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If an end of model year report contains the statement
  referred to in subparagraph (2)(b)(vi) for a given model year, the
  company must provide in its end of model year report the number of
  Class 7 and Class 8 vocational tractors that conform to the emission
  standards applicable to vocational vehicles in accordance with section
  28 and that it manufactures or imports for that model year and for the
  two previous model years.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional information --- emission
  credit transfers

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} The end of model year report must also contain the
  following information for each CO~2~ emission credit transfer and
  early action credit transfer to or from the company since the
  submission of the previous end of model year report:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, street address and, if different, the
    mailing address of the company that transferred the credits and the
    model year for which that company obtained those credits;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name, street address and, if different, the
    mailing address of the company that received the credits;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date of the transfer; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the number of credits transferred, expressed in
    megagrams of CO~2~.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-186, s. 65
- SOR/2018-98, ss. 52, 60
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-48-20150716.html "Link to previous version of section 48"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Early Action Credits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-802184 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents

- **[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} To
  obtain early action credits under section 47, a company must include
  in its 2014 or 2016 end of model year report, as the case may be, the
  following information for each averaging set of the 2011 to 2013 model
  years or of the 2015 model year, as the case may be:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for each fleet of Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty
    vehicles and cab-complete vehicles, excluding those that are
    vocational vehicles or incomplete vocational vehicles,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the number of credits or deficits calculated in
      accordance with paragraph 35(1)(a),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the N~2~O and CH~4~ emission standards
      applicable to each fleet,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the fleet average CO~2~ emission standard,
      determined for A in the formula set out in paragraph 35(1)(a),

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ emission target value for each vehicle
      subconfiguration of each fleet, determined for A in the formula
      set out in subsection 22(1),

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the work factor for each vehicle subconfiguration
      calculated in accordance with subsection 22(3),

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the GVWR, curb weight, GCWR, type of
      transmission, gear ratio, axle ratio and type of engine for each
      vehicle configuration,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the fleet average CO~2~ emission value,
      determined for B in the formula set out in paragraph 35(1)(a),

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ emission value for each vehicle
      configuration, determined for A in the formula set out in
      subsection 23(1) and taking into account subsection 23(2),

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the N~2~O or CH~4~ family
      emission limit, determined for B in the formula set out in
      subsection 20(3),

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} the number of vehicles of each vehicle
      configuration and subconfiguration,

    - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} the number of vehicles in each fleet, and

    - [(xii)]{.lawlabel} the number of vehicles in the averaging set;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for each fleet of vocational vehicles and
    incomplete vocational vehicles,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the number of credits or deficits for each fleet
      and subfleet, calculated in accordance with paragraph 35(1)(b),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ emission standard that applies to the
      vehicles of each fleet or subfleet, as the case may be, determined
      for A in the formula set out in paragraph 35(1)(b),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ family emission limit for each fleet
      or subfleet, as the case may be, determined for B in the formula
      set out in paragraph 35(1)(b), and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the number of those vehicles in each averaging
      set, fleet and subfleet;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for each fleet of tractors and incomplete tractors,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the number of credits or deficits for each fleet
      and subfleet, calculated in accordance with paragraph 35(1)(c),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ emission standard that applies to the
      vehicles of each fleet or subfleet, as the case may be, determined
      for A in the formula set out in paragraph 35(1)(c),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ family emission limit for each fleet
      or subfleet, as the case may be, determined for B in the formula
      set out in paragraph 35(1)(c), and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the number of tractors and incomplete tractors
      in each averaging set, fleet and subfleet;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for each fleet of heavy-duty engines,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the number of credits or deficits for each fleet,
      calculated in accordance with paragraph 35(1)(d),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the N~2~O and CH~4~ emission standards that
      apply to the engines of each fleet,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ emission standard that applies to the
      engines of each fleet, determined for A in the formula set out in
      paragraph 35(1)(d),

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ deteriorated emission level value for
      each fleet, determined for B in the formula set out in paragraph
      35(1)(d), and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the number of engines in each averaging set,
      fleet and engine configuration; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an identification of every instance in each fleet
    or subfleet, as the case may be, when the 1.5 credit multiplier was
    used in accordance with subsection 47(6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional credits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} To obtain additional early action credits under
  section 47, a company must include in its 2014 or 2016 end of model
  year report, as the case may be, the values referred to in paragraphs
  48(7)(q) to (t).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 60
:::
