Version](section-49-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 49"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Format of Reports]{.HTitleText2} {#h-802222 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submission

**[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** Any report to be
submitted under these Regulations must be submitted electronically in
the format provided by the Minister, but the report must be submitted in
writing if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} no such format is provided; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is, owing to circumstances beyond the control of
  the person required to submit the report, impracticable to submit the
  report electronically in the format provided.

## [Documentation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-802227 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Engine installation

- **[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company that manufactures or imports a heavy-duty engine must ensure
  that every engine that is installed in a vehicle in Canada is
  accompanied by documentation respecting the engine and emission
  controls or by the address of the place or the website where that
  documentation may be obtained.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The documentation must contain the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} detailed installation procedures for the exhaust
    system, emission control system, aftertreatment devices and their
    components;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all necessary steps for installing any diagnostic
    system required under part 86 of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter C,
    of the CFR;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the limits on the types of use for the engine to
    ensure that the emission standards set out in these Regulations are
    complied with; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for each engine configuration, the fuel map results
    and other information described in section 503 of Title 40, chapter
    I, subchapter U, part 1036, subpart F, of the CFR.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Language

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The documentation must be provided in English, French
  or both official languages, as requested by the installer. However, in
  the case of a fuel map input file used for running the GEM computer
  simulation model, if the installer requests that the fuel map input
  file be provided in French, it must be provided in both official
  languages.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 53
- [SOR/2022-204, s. 10](#1376002-1377439){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1376002-1377439.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-51-20181116.html "Link to previous version of section 51"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tire maintenance

- **[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In the
  case of vocational vehicles, tractors and trailers, a company must
  ensure that the documentation respecting tire maintenance and
  replacement is provided to the first retail purchaser of every vehicle
  or trailer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Language

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The documentation must be provided in English, French
  or both official languages, as requested by the purchaser.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 53
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-52-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 52"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Records]{.HTitleText1} {#h-802248 .Part}

### [Evidence of Conformity]{.HTitleText2} {#h-802249 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vehicle, engine or trailer covered by EPA
certificate

**[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** For a heavy-duty vehicle,
heavy-duty engine or trailer that is covered by an EPA certificate and
that is sold concurrently in Canada and the United States or has a
national emissions mark applied to it, evidence of conformity in respect
of a company for the purposes of paragraph 153(1)(b) of the Act consists
of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the EPA certificate covering the vehicle,
  the engine or the trailer and, if applicable, a copy of the evidence
  of the EPA approval concerning the vehicle, the engine or the trailer
  as referred to in paragraph 27(6)(a) or 33.1(4)(a), paragraph (a) of
  the description of A in subsection 40(1) or paragraph 41(2)(a), as the
  case may be;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} one or both of the following:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a document demonstrating that the vehicles, the
    engines or the trailers covered by the EPA certificate are sold
    concurrently in Canada and the United States,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a document demonstrating that the vehicle, the
    engine or the trailer covered by the EPA certificate bears the
    national emissions mark;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the records submitted to the EPA in support
  of the application for the EPA certificate in respect of the vehicle,
  engine or trailer and any application for an amendment to that EPA
  certificate and any records submitted to the EPA to maintain that EPA
  certificate; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a U.S. emission control information label or, in the
  case of a heavy-duty engine, a U.S. engine information label that is
  permanently affixed to the vehicle, the engine or the trailer in the
  form and location set out in

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} section 35 of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter C,
    part 86, subpart A, of the CFR, section 1807 of Title 40, chapter I,
    subchapter C, part 86, subpart S, of the CFR and section 135 of
    Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1037, subpart B, of the CFR,
    for the applicable model year of the heavy-duty vehicle or trailer,
    and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} section 35 of Title 40, chapter I, subchapter C,
    part 86, subpart A, of the CFR and section 135 of Title 40, chapter
    I, subchapter U, part 1036, subpart B, of the CFR, for the
    applicable model year of the heavy-duty engine.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-186, s. 66
- SOR/2018-98, s. 53
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-53-20150716.html "Link to previous version of section 53"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Paragraph 153(1)(b) of Act

- **[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of paragraph 153(1)(b) of the Act, a company must obtain
  and produce evidence of conformity for a heavy-duty vehicle,
  heavy-duty engine or trailer --- other than one referred to in section
  53 --- in a form and manner satisfactory to the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submission of evidence of conformity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 55, a company must submit the
  evidence of conformity to the Minister before importing a heavy-duty
  vehicle, heavy-duty engine or trailer or applying a national emissions
  mark to it.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 53
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-54-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 54"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsection 153(2) of Act

**[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty, a
company that imports a heavy-duty vehicle, heavy-duty engine or trailer
or applies a national emissions mark to it under subsection 153(2) of
the Act must submit the evidence of conformity referred to in subsection
54(1) to the Minister before the vehicle, the engine or the trailer
leaves the possession or control of the company and, in the case of a
vehicle, before it is presented for registration under the laws of a
province or of an Aboriginal government.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 53
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-55-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 55"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Fleet Average Emissions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-802273 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents

- **[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company that participates in the CO~2~ emission credit system must
  maintain records containing the following information for each of its
  fleets:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for each fleet of Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty
    vehicles and cab-complete vehicles, excluding those that are
    vocational vehicles or incomplete vocational vehicles,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the model year,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the fleet average CO~2~ emission standard,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the fleet average CO~2~ emission value and, if
      applicable, the N~2~O and CH~4~ emission values,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the values and data used in calculating the
      fleet average CO~2~ emission standard and the fleet average CO~2~
      emission value and, if applicable, in calculating the N~2~O and
      CH~4~ emission values,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the values and data used in calculating the
      number of CO~2~ emission credits and, if applicable, the number of
      early action credits,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the number of CO~2~ emission credits used to
      offset a N~2~O or CH~4~ emission deficit, if applicable, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the GVWR, curb weight, GCWR, type of
      transmission, gear ratio, axle ratio and type of engine for each
      vehicle configuration;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for each fleet and, if applicable, subfleet of
    vocational vehicles and incomplete vocational vehicles,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the model year,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the applicable CO~2~ emission standard,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ emission rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the values and data, including the GEM computer
      simulation model inputs and results, used in calculating the CO~2~
      emission rate, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the values and data used in calculating the
      number of CO~2~ emission credits and, if applicable, the number of
      early action credits;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for each fleet and, if applicable, subfleet of
    tractors and incomplete tractors,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the model year,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the applicable CO~2~ emission standard,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ emission rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the values and data, including the GEM computer
      simulation model inputs and results, used in calculating the CO~2~
      emission rate, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the values and data used in calculating the
      number of CO~2~ emission credits and, if applicable, the number of
      early action credits;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for each fleet of heavy-duty engines,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the model year,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the applicable CO~2~ emission standard,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ deteriorated emission level value,
      and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the values and data used in calculating the
      number of CO~2~ emission credits and, if applicable, the number of
      early action credits; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} for each fleet and, if applicable, subfleet of
    full-aero box van trailers,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the model year,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the applicable CO~2~ emission standard,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ emission rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the values and data, including the inputs and
      result of the compliance equation set out in section 515(a) of
      Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1037, subpart F, of the
      CFR, used in calculating the CO~2~ emission rate, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the values and data used in calculating the
      number of CO~2~ emission credits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents --- heavy-duty vehicles

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company must maintain records containing the
  following information for each heavy-duty vehicle in the fleets
  referred to in paragraphs (1)(a) to (c):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the model year and vehicle configuration or
    subfleet of the vehicle, as the case may be;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ emission standard that applies to the
    vehicles of each subfleet and the fleet average CO~2~ emission
    standard;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for a vehicle that is covered by an EPA
    certificate, the vehicle family or the applicable test group;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name and street address of the plant where the
    vehicle was assembled;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle identification number;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ emission value that applies to the fleet
    of the vehicle or the CO~2~ emission rate that applies to the
    subfleet of the vehicle, as the case may be, and the values and data
    used in calculating that value or rate; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the name and the street or mailing address of the
    first retail purchaser of the vehicle in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents --- engines

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company must maintain records containing the
  following information for each heavy-duty engine in the fleets
  referred to in paragraph (1)(d):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the model year, the engine configuration and the
    fleet of the engine;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date of manufacture;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the gross power;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the identification of the emission control system;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ emission standard that applies to the
    engines of the fleet;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the engine family's identification code;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the name of the engine manufacturer;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the unique identification number of the engine;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the deterioration factor and whether it constitutes
    a multiplicative deterioration factor or an additive deterioration
    factor, and the values and data used in calculating that factor; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the name and the street or mailing address of the
    first retail purchaser of the engine in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents --- trailers

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A company must maintain records containing the
  following information for each of its full-aero box van trailers in
  the fleets referred to in paragraph (1)(e):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the model year;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the type of trailer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ emission standard that applies to the
    type of trailer;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for a trailer that is covered by an EPA
    certificate, the trailer family's identification code;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name and street address of the plant where the
    trailer was manufactured;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the trailer's identification number;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the CO~2~ emission rate that applies to the trailer
    and the values and data used in calculating that rate; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the name and the street or mailing address of the
    first retail purchaser of the trailer in Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, ss. 54, 60
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-56-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 56"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Engines Sold Concurrently]{.HTitleText2} {#h-802341 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence of number of engines sold

**[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** If subsection 13(7.1) or
(8) applies in respect of a company's heavy-duty engines, the company
must maintain records demonstrating the number of heavy-duty engines
sold in the United States that are of the same engine family.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 55
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-57-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 57"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Vocational Tractors]{.HTitleText2} {#h-802346 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vocational tractor

**[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
section 28, in the case of a tractor that conforms to the emission
standards applicable to vocational vehicles instead of those applicable
to tractors, the company must maintain records demonstrating that the
tractor is a vocational tractor.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 56
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-58-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 58"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Maintenance and Submission of Records]{.HTitleText2} {#h-802351 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maintenance of records

- **[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  heavy-duty vehicles, heavy-duty engines and trailers, a company must
  maintain in writing or in a readily readable electronic or optical
  form for each model year

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the reports referred to in sections 48
    and 49 for a period of at least eight years after the end of the
    calendar year that corresponds to the model year;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the evidence of conformity and records referred to
    in sections 53 and 54 for a period of at least eight years after the
    day on which the main assembly of the vehicle or manufacture of the
    engine or the trailer was completed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the records referred to in sections 56 and 57 for a
    period of at least eight years after the end of the calendar year
    that corresponds to the model year; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the records referred to in section 58 for a period
    of at least three years after the end of the calendar year that
    corresponds to the model year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Records maintained on behalf of company

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the copy of the reports, the evidence of
  conformity and the records referred to in subsection (1) are
  maintained on behalf of a company, the company must keep a record of
  the name, street address and, if different, the mailing address of the
  person who maintains those records.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time limits

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister makes a written request for the
  evidence of conformity or the records referred to in subsections (1)
  and (2), or a summary of any of them, the company must provide the
  Minister with the requested information, in either official language,
  within

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 40 days after the day on which the request is
    delivered to the company; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the evidence of conformity or records referred
    to in section 53 or 54 must be translated from a language other than
    French or English, 60 days after the day on which the request is
    delivered to the company.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 57
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-59-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 59"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Information Regarding Suspension or Revocation of EPA Certificate]{.HTitleText1} {#h-802367 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information to be submitted

**[[59.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59.1 .sectionLabel}** If an EPA certificate
referred to in section 13 is suspended or revoked, the company must
submit the following information to the Minister within 60 days after
the day on which the certificate is suspended or revoked:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} its name, address and telephone number;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the EPA certificate that was suspended or
  revoked;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the EPA decision to suspend or revoke the
  certificate; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the make, model, if applicable, and model year of the
  vehicles, engines and trailers that are covered by the EPA
  certificate.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-186, s. 67
- SOR/2018-98, s. 58
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-59.1-20150716.html "Link to previous version of section 59.1"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Importation Document]{.HTitleText1} {#h-802376 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Importation for exhibition,
demonstration, evaluation or testing

- **[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  declaration referred to in paragraph 155(1)(a) of the Act must be
  signed by the person referred to in that paragraph or their authorized
  representative and must contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the importer's name, email address, telephone
    number, street address and, if different, mailing address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the manufacturer of the vehicle, engine
    or trailer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the expected date of the importation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle, the vehicle's class,
    make, model, model year and identification number;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an engine, a description of the
    engine;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a trailer, the trailer's make, model
    year, identification number and model or, if there is no model, its
    type, length and axle configuration;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the vehicle, engine or trailer
    will be used in Canada solely for purposes of exhibition,
    demonstration, evaluation or testing; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the vehicle, engine or trailer
    will be removed from Canada or destroyed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing of declaration

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The declaration must be filed with the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} before the import of the vehicle, engine or
    trailer; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a company whose annual world
    production of vehicles, engines or trailers is 2500 or more, as
    described in paragraph (a) or quarterly.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 59
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-60-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 60"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Rental Rate]{.HTitleText1} {#h-802394 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual rental rate

**[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** The annual rental rate to
be paid to a company by the Minister under subsection 159(1) of the Act,
prorated on a daily basis for each day that a vehicle, engine or trailer
is made available, is 21% of the manufacturer's suggested retail price
of the vehicle, engine or trailer.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 59
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-61-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 61"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Application for Exemption]{.HTitleText1} {#h-802399 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

- **[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company applying under section 156 of the Act for an exemption from
  conformity with any standard prescribed under these Regulations must,
  before importing a vehicle or engine or applying a national emissions
  mark to a vehicle or engine, submit in writing to the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its name, street address and, if different, its
    mailing address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the province or country under the laws of which it
    is established;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the section number, title and text or substance of
    the standards from which an exemption is sought;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the duration requested for the exemption;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the reason for requesting an exemption; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the company is requesting that the information
    submitted be treated as confidential under section 313 of the Act,
    the reasons for the request.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- paragraph 156(1)(a) of
  the Act

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the basis of an application for an exemption is
  the substantial financial hardship referred to in paragraph 156(1)(a)
  of the Act, the company must include in the submission to the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the world production of vehicles or engines
    manufactured by the company or by the manufacturer of the model that
    is the subject of the application in the 12-month period beginning
    two years before the beginning of the period in respect of which the
    exemption is sought;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the total number of vehicles or engines
    manufactured for, or imported into, the Canadian market in the
    12-month period beginning two years before the beginning of the
    period in respect of which the exemption is sought;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} technical and financial information demonstrating
    in detail why conformity to the standards referred to in paragraph
    (1)(c) would create substantial financial hardship, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a list of each of the items that would have to be
      altered in order to achieve conformity,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an itemized and detailed description of the
      estimated cost to make the alterations referred to in subparagraph
      (i)

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} at the end of one year after the day on which
        the application is submitted, if the duration of the requested
        exemption is for a period of one year or more but less than two
        years,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} at the end of two years after the day on which
        the application is submitted, if the duration of the requested
        exemption is for a period of two years or more but less than
        three years, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} at the end of three years after the day on
        which the application is submitted, if the duration of the
        requested exemption is for a period of three years,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the estimated price increase per vehicle or
      engine to counter the total costs incurred under subparagraph (ii)
      and a statement of the anticipated effect of each such price
      increase, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} corporate balance sheets and income statements
      for the three fiscal years before the filing of the application;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a description of the company's efforts to have its
    vehicles or engines conform to the standards from which the
    exemption is sought, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a description of any other means of achieving
      conformity that were considered and the reasons for rejecting each
      of them, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the steps to be taken while the
      exemption is in effect and the estimated date by which conformity
      will be achieved through design changes to or the termination of
      the production of non-conforming vehicles or engines; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the research, development and testing
    documentation establishing that an exemption would not substantially
    diminish the control of emissions of the vehicle or the engine,
    including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the reasons why not conforming to the prescribed
      standards would not substantially diminish the control of
      emissions of the vehicle or the engine, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a description of other means of achieving
      conformity with the prescribed standards that were considered by
      the company and the reasons for rejecting each of them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- paragraph 156(1)(b) of
  Act

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the basis of an application for an exemption is
  the development of new emission monitoring or emission control
  features referred to in paragraph 156(1)(b) of the Act, the company
  must include in the submission to the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the new features;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the research, development and testing
    documentation establishing the innovative nature of the new
    features;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an analysis of how the level of performance of the
    new features is equivalent or superior to the level of performance
    required by the prescribed standards, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a detailed description of how a vehicle or engine
      equipped with the new features would, if exempted, differ from a
      vehicle or engine that conforms to the prescribed standards, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the results of tests conducted on the new
      features that demonstrate a level of performance that is
      equivalent or superior to that required by the prescribed
      standards;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} evidence that an exemption would facilitate the
    development or the field evaluation of the vehicle or the engine;
    and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a statement as to whether the company intends, at
    the end of the exemption period,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to have the vehicle or the engine conform to the
      prescribed standards,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to apply for a further exemption, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} to request that the prescribed standards be
      amended to incorporate the new features.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- paragraph 156(1)(c) of
  Act

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the basis of an application for an exemption is
  the development of new kinds of vehicles, engines or vehicle or engine
  systems or components referred to in paragraph 156(1)(c) of the Act,
  the company must include in the submission to the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the research, development and testing
    documentation establishing that an exemption would not substantially
    diminish the control of emissions of the vehicle or the engine,
    including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a detailed description of how the vehicle or the
      engine equipped with the new kinds of vehicle systems or
      components would, if exempted, differ from one that conforms to
      the prescribed standards,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the reasons why not conforming to the prescribed
      standards would not substantially diminish the control of
      emissions of the vehicle or the engine, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a description of other means of achieving
      conformity that were considered and the reasons for rejecting each
      of them;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} evidence that an exemption would facilitate the
    development or the field evaluation of the vehicle or the engine;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement as to whether the company intends, at
    the end of the exemption period, to have the vehicle or the engine
    conform to the prescribed standards.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New exemption

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a company wishes to obtain a new exemption to take
  effect after the expiry of an exemption referred to in subsection (3)
  or (4), the company must submit, in writing, to the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information required under subsection (3) or
    (4), as the case may be; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a statement of the total number of vehicles or
    engines sold in Canada under the expiring exemption.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-98, s. 59
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-62-20130222.html "Link to previous version of section 62"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Defect Information]{.HTitleText1} {#h-802453 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of defect

- **[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  notice of defect referred to in subsections 157(1) and (4) of the Act
  must contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the company giving the notice and its
    street address and, if different, mailing address, and the name,
    email address, telephone number and, if any, facsimile number of the
    appropriate contact person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of vehicles, the range or ranges of
    vehicle identification numbers for the vehicles in respect of which
    the notice is given, if known, and, for each of those vehicles, its
    make, model and model year and the period during which it was
    manufactured;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of engines, for each engine in respect
    of which the notice is given, its make, model and model year and the
    period during which it was manufactured;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of trailers, the range or ranges of
    trailer identification numbers for the trailers in respect of which
    the notice is given, if known, and, for each of those trailers,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} its make,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} its model or, if there is no model, its type,
      length and axle configuration,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} its model year, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the period during which it was manufactured;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the total number of vehicles, engines or trailers
    in respect of which the notice is given or, if the total number is
    not known, the estimated number, and the number or estimated number
    of vehicles, engines or trailers in each identifying classification;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the estimated percentage of the potentially
    affected vehicles, engines or trailers that contain the defect;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a description of the defect;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} an evaluation of the pollution risk arising from
    the defect;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a statement of the measures to be taken to correct
    the defect; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a chronology of the principal events that led to
    the determination of the existence of the defect, if known.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice of defect must be given in writing and,
  when given to a person other than the Minister, must be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in both official languages; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the person's official language of choice, if
    known.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Initial report

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company must, within 60 days after the day on which
  a notice of defect is given, submit to the Minister the initial report
  referred to in subsection 157(7) of the Act containing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any changes to the information referred to in
    subsection (1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if not already provided in the notice, the range or
    ranges of vehicle or trailer identification numbers;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if not already provided in the notice, the total
    number of vehicles, engines or trailers in respect of which the
    notice was given and the number of vehicles, engines or trailers in
    each identifying classification;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if not already provided in the notice, a chronology
    of the principal events that led to the determination of the
    existence of the defect; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} copies of all notices, bulletins and other
    circulars issued by the company in respect of the defect, including
    a detailed description of the nature and physical location of the
    defect with diagrams and other illustrations, as necessary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quarterly reports

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a company submits an initial report under
  subsection (3), it must, within 45 days after the day on which each
  quarter ends, submit quarterly reports to the Minister respecting the
  defect and its correction that contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number, title or other identification assigned
    by the company to the notice of defect;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the revised number of vehicles,
    engines or trailers in respect of which the notice of defect was
    given;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the notice of defect was given to
    the current owners of the affected vehicles, engines or trailers and
    the dates of any follow-up communications with those owners in
    respect of the notice; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the total number or percentage of vehicles, engines
    or trailers repaired by or on behalf of the company, including
    vehicles, engines or trailers requiring inspection only.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applicable standard --- CO~2~ emissions

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the application of section 157 of the Act, the
  CO~2~ emission standard that applies

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to Class 2B and Class 3 heavy-duty vehicles and
    cab-complete vehicles --- excluding those that are vocational
    vehicles or incomplete vocational vehicles --- or to spark-ignition
    engines that conform to the alternative CO~2~ emission standard
    referred to in section 25 is the product of 1.1 multiplied by the
    CO~2~ emission value for that vehicle configuration, rounded to the
    nearest gram per mile;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to vocational vehicles is the result of the GEM
    computer simulation model using the parameters specified in
    subsection 26(2);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to tractors of the 2020 model year or an earlier
    model year is the result of the GEM computer simulation model using
    the parameters specified in subsection 27(2), except that the
    coefficient of aerodynamic drag may originate from a bin for which
    the coefficient of aerodynamic drag is higher than that of the bin
    of the subject vehicle;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to tractors of the 2021 model year or a subsequent
    model year is the result of the GEM computer simulation model using
    the parameters specified in subsection 27(2);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} to heavy-duty engines --- other than engines
    referred to in paragraph (f), (g) or (h) --- is the product of 1.03
    multiplied by the applicable standard set out in section 30 for that
    engine, or in the case of an engine that is grouped into a fleet
    referred to in section 18, the product of 1.03 multiplied by the
    CO~2~ deteriorated emission level applicable to the fleet;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} to heavy-duty engines of the 2014 to 2016 model
    years that conform to the alternative CO~2~ emission standard
    referred to in subsection 31(1) is the product of 1.03 multiplied by
    the alternative CO~2~ emission standard;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} to heavy-duty engines of the 2013 to 2016 model
    years that conform to the alternative CO~2~ emission standard
    referred to in subsection 31(2) is the product of 1.03 multiplied by
    the alternative CO~2~ emission standard;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} to heavy-duty engines of the 2024 to 2026 model
    years that conform to the alternative CO~2~ emission standard
    referred to in subsection 31(5) is the product of 1.03 multiplied by
    the alternative CO~2~ emission standard, or in the case of an engine
    that is grouped into a fleet referred to in section 18, the product
    of 1.03 multiplied by the CO~2~ deteriorated emission level
    applicable to the fleet; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to full-aero and partial-aero box van trailers is
    the result of the compliance equation set out in section 515(a) of
    Title 40, chapter I, subchapter U, part 1037, subpart F, of the CFR.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applicable standard --- design

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of section 157 of the Act, the
  standard that applies to a non-box trailer or a non-aero box van
  trailer is the one that is set out in section 16.1.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-186, s. 68
- SOR/2018-98, s. 59
:::

[Previous
Version](section-63-20150716.html "Link to previous version of section 63"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration

**[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations come
into force on the day on which they are registered.
